Motivation: 基础模型在开放世界部署中面临的知识边界、能力上限、组合性偏移和开放式任务变化等OOD现象，与以往OOD研究中的设定存在本质差异，且预训练和后训练分布往往仅被部分观测，现有模型中心范式无法应对这些挑战。

Method: 1) 提出阶段感知的OOD形式化框架，容纳部分观测的多阶段训练分布；2) 证明参数覆盖上限定理：存在模型中心方法（无论是训练时还是测试时）在容忍度ε内无法处理的实践相关输入；3) 刻画智能体OOD系统的四大结构性质：感知、策略选择、外部动作和闭环验证；4) 回应七项反驳观点并承认其中两项。

Result: 证明存在参数覆盖上限，即某些实践相关输入是任何模型中心方法（基于参数表示的本质原因）都无法在容忍度ε内处理的；智能体系统通过四大结构性质严格扩展了可达集，超越了这一上限。

Conclusion: 模型中心方法与智能体方法是互补的，而非替代关系；基础模型的OOD研究需要明确承认智能体范式作为一等研究方向的地位。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) are increasingly deployed in open-world settings where distribution shift is the rule rather than the exception. The out-of-distribution (OOD) phenomena they face -- knowledge boundaries, capability ceilings, compositional shifts, and open-ended task variation -- differ in kind from the settings that have shaped prior OOD research, and are further complicated because the pretraining and post-training distributions of modern FMs are often only partially observed. Our position is that OOD for foundation models is a structurally distinct problem that cannot be solved within the prevailing model-centric paradigm, and that agentic systems constitute the missing paradigm required to address it. We defend this claim through four steps. First, we give a stage-aware formalization of OOD that accommodates partially observed multi-stage training distributions. Second, we prove a parameter coverage ceiling: there exist practically relevant inputs that no model-centric method (training-time or test-time) can handle within tolerance $\varepsilon$, for reasons intrinsic to parameter-based representation. Third, we characterize agentic OOD systems by four structural properties -- perception, strategy selection, external action, and closed-loop verification -- and show that they strictly extend the reachable set beyond the ceiling. Fourth, we respond to seven counterarguments, conceding two, and outline a research agenda. We do not claim that agentic methods subsume model-centric ones; we argue that the two are complementary, and that progress on FM-OOD requires explicit recognition of the agentic paradigm as a first-class research direction.

</details>


### [255] [Memory Inception: Latent-Space KV Cache Manipulation for Steering LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06225)
*Andy Zeyi Liu,Michael Zhang,Ilana Greenberg,Adam Alnasser,Lucas Baker,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出记忆植入（Memory Inception, MI），一种无需训练的潜注意力空间引导方法，通过在选定层插入文本导出的KV缓存实现选择性引导，在人格引导、可更新引导和结构化推理任务上优于现有方法，同时大幅降低KV存储开销（最高118倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引导方法存在两难：指令提示控制力强但缓存负担重且混乱长交互；激活引导虽紧凑但控制力弱且不支持大规模结构化提示。需要一种兼顾控制力与存储效率的引导方法。

Method: 记忆植入（MI）是一种无需训练的方法，在潜注意力空间中通过仅在选定层插入文本导出的键值（KV）缓存来实施引导，将引导视为选择性KV分配，仅在模型路由到的位置注入潜变量槽位。

Result: 在人格引导任务上，MI实现了最佳的控制-漂移权衡，与提示方法竞争力相当且持续优于CAA；在可更新引导上，支持对话中行为切换，在Qwen3上实现最高后切换对齐度；在结构化推理上，在HARDMath和PHYSICS上10/12个subject×mode单元格优于可见提示，同时将内容匹配的KV存储减少高达118倍。

Conclusion: MI是一种强大的引导方法，特别适用于引导信息是持久性、结构化或放在可见提示中代价高昂的场景。

Abstract: Steering large language models (LLMs) is usually done by either instruction prompting or activation steering. Prompting often gives strong control, but caches guidance tokens at every layer and can clutter long interactions; activation steering is compact but typically weaker and does not support large structured reminders. We introduce memory inception (MI), a training-free method that steers in latent attention space by inserting text-derived key-value (KV) banks only at selected layers. Rather than materializing reminder content throughout the prompt cache, MI treats steering as selective KV allocation, injecting latent slots only where the model routes to them. On matched personality-steering tasks, MI gives the best overall control--drift trade-off, remaining competitive with prompting while consistently outperforming CAA. On updateable guidance, MI supports mid-conversation behavior shifts without rewriting the visible transcript, achieving the highest post-shift alignment on Qwen3. On structured reasoning, MI outperforms visible prompting on HARDMath and PHYSICS (10/12 subject$\times$mode cells), serving as proxies for structured reasoning in verifiable domains, while cutting content-matched KV storage by up to 118$\times$. These results position MI as a powerful steering method when guidance is persistent, structured, or expensive to keep in the visible transcript.

</details>


### [256] [Soft Deterministic Policy Gradient with Gaussian Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06228)
*Hyunjun Na,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对确定性策略梯度(DPG)在非平滑奖励函数场景下因依赖评论家动作梯度导致策略梯度定义不良的问题，提出基于高斯平滑的平滑Bellman方程，推导软确定性策略梯度(Soft-DPG)，消除对评论家动作梯度的显式依赖，实例化为Soft DDPG算法。实验表明在稠密奖励场景具有竞争力，在离散化奖励场景显著优于标准DDPG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPG依赖评论家对动作的可微性，但在稀疏或离散奖励等实际控制问题中，该假设被违反，导致策略梯度定义不良且学习不稳定。因此需要一种不依赖评论家动作梯度的策略梯度方法。

Method: 基于高斯平滑构造平滑Bellman方程，定义新的动作-价值函数，推导软确定性策略梯度(Soft-DPG)，使梯度在非光滑Q函数下仍良好定义，并实例化为Soft DDPG深度强化学习算法。

Result: 在标准连续控制基准和离散化奖励变体上，Soft DDPG在稠密奖励场景保持竞争力，在大多数离散化奖励环境中相比标准DDPG取得明显提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的Soft-DPG框架有效解决了DPG在非光滑奖励场景下的梯度定义问题，在不牺牲稠密奖励性能的前提下，显著提高了离散化奖励环境下的学习稳定性与效果。

Abstract: Deterministic policy gradient (DPG) is widely utilized for continuous control; however, it inherently relies on the differentiability of the critic with respect to the action during policy updates. This assumption is violated in practical control problems involving sparse or discrete rewards, leading to ill-defined policy gradients and unstable learning. To address these challenges, we propose a principled alternative based on a smoothed Bellman equation formulated via Gaussian smoothing. Specifically, we define a novel action-value function based on a smoothed Bellman equation and derive the soft deterministic policy gradient (Soft-DPG). Our formulation eliminates explicit dependence on critic action-gradients and ensures that the gradient remains well-defined even for non-smooth Q-functions. We instantiate this framework into a deep reinforcement learning algorithm, which we call soft deep deterministic policy gradient (Soft DDPG). Empirical evaluations on standard continuous control benchmarks and their discretized-reward variants show that Soft DDPG remains competitive in dense-reward settings and provides clear gains in most discretized-reward environments, where standard DDPG is more sensitive to irregular critic landscapes.

</details>


### [257] [SoftSAE: Dynamic Top-K Selection for Adaptive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06610)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoftSAE提出了一种动态Top-K选择机制的稀疏自编码器，通过学习输入依赖的稀疏水平k，使模型能根据不同输入复杂度自适应调整激活特征数量，从而更好地匹配数据结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TopK SAE等架构使用固定稀疏度K，忽略了真实世界数据局部内在维度的变化——简单输入仅需少量特征，复杂输入需要更多表达，固定K会导致简单情况下引入噪声或复杂情况下丢失重要结构。

Method: 使用可微分的Soft Top-K算子学习输入依赖的稀疏水平k，实现动态调整激活特征数量，使稀疏度随输入复杂度自适应变化。

Result: 实验结果表明SoftSAE不仅能找到有意义的特征，还能为每个概念选择恰当数量的特征。

Conclusion: 动态稀疏水平能更好地匹配数据的流形结构，解释长度反映输入信息量，优于固定稀疏度方法。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool in mechanistic interpretability, helping to analyze internal representations in both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs). By decomposing polysemantic activations into sparse sets of monosemantic features, SAEs aim to translate neural network computations into human-understandable concepts. However, common architectures such as TopK SAEs rely on a fixed sparsity level. They enforce the same number of active features (K) across all inputs, ignoring the varying complexity of real-world data. Natural data often lies on manifolds with varying local intrinsic dimensionality, meaning the number of relevant factors can change significantly across samples. This suggests that a fixed sparsity level is not optimal. Simple inputs may require only a few features, while more complex ones need more expressive representations. Using a constant K can therefore introduce noise in simple cases or miss important structure in more complex ones. To address this issue, we propose SoftSAE, a sparse autoencoder with a Dynamic Top-K selection mechanism. Our method uses a differentiable Soft Top-K operator to learn an input-dependent sparsity level k. This allows the model to adjust the number of active features based on the complexity of each input. As a result, the representation better matches the structure of the data, and the explanation length reflects the amount of information in the input. Experimental results confirm that SoftSAE not only finds meaningful features, but also selects the right number of features for each concept. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/.

</details>


### [258] [Band Together: Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination against Evasion-based Promotion Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06238)
*Guanmeng Xian,Ning Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UAT-MC方法，针对多模态推荐系统中逃避型推广攻击的跨模态梯度不匹配问题，通过梯度对齐机制实现跨模态扰动同步，提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推荐系统利用视觉和文本信号缓解数据稀疏性，但同时也更容易受到逃避型推广攻击。现有防御主要针对单模态设置和投毒型威胁，对逃避型威胁的探索不足。本文首先发现在多用户推广场景下存在跨模态梯度不匹配现象，视觉和文本扰动因不同用户群体的主导而朝不一致方向优化，削弱了攻击有效性并导致鲁棒训练低估最坏情况风险。

Method: 提出UAT-MC（面向多模态协调的未目标对抗训练）：1）将所有物品视为潜在目标，解决逃避型攻击中目标物品未知的挑战；2）引入梯度对齐机制显式修正跨模态梯度不匹配，确保跨模态扰动同步，最大化对抗强度以进行鲁棒训练。

Result: 大量实验表明，UAT-MC在防御-准确率权衡下，显著提升了对推广攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持可接受的推荐性能。

Conclusion: UAT-MC有效解决了多模态推荐系统中逃避型推广攻击的防御难题，通过跨模态梯度对齐实现了同步扰动，为多模态鲁棒推荐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal recommender systems exploit visual and textual signals to alleviate data sparsity, but this also makes them more vulnerable to evasion-based promotion attacks. Existing defenses are largely limited to single-modal settings and mainly focus on poisoning-based threats, leaving evasion-based threats underexplored. In this work, we first identify a cross-modal gradient mismatch under the multi-user promotion setting, where visual and textual perturbations are optimized in inconsistent directions due to the dominance of distinct user groups. This phenomenon dilutes the attack effectiveness and leads robust training to underestimate worst-case risks. To address this issue, we propose Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination (UAT-MC). UAT-MC tackles the challenge of unknown targeted items in evasion-based attacks (as opposed to poisoning-based attacks) by treating all items as potential targets, and introduces a gradient alignment mechanism to explicitly correct this mismatch. This design ensures synchronized perturbations across modalities, thereby maximizing adversarial strength for robust training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UAT-MC significantly improves robustness against promotion attacks while maintaining acceptable recommendation performance under the defense-accuracy trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/gmXian/UAT-MC.

</details>


### [259] [When Graph Language Models Go Beyond Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06239)
*Masatsugu Yamada,Mahito Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种校准诊断协议，结合频繁子图挖掘、图级bootstrap基线和三级频率分层，来区分图语言模型是真正学到了结构规律还是仅仅记住了训练图。实验表明，大规模模型确实能学习结构规律（主要在高频区域），但小规模模型的表现与记忆难以区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的聚合保真度指标无法区分图语言模型是学习了结构规律还是仅仅记忆了训练图，需要更精细的诊断方法来解耦记忆与结构对齐。

Method: 开发了包含频繁子图挖掘、图级bootstrap基线和三级频率分层的校准诊断协议，在五个TU基准数据集上对LLaMA风格的图语言模型进行分析。

Result: 1) 小规模下模型与记忆难以区分；2) 大规模（375万图）下逐字记忆显著下降，但秩相关性保持高位；3) 高频模式被良好复现，稀有模式覆盖差；4) 在两种图序列化下结果稳健。

Conclusion: 图语言模型在一定规模下可以学习到超越记忆的结构规律（主要体现在高频区域），但小规模下主要表现为记忆，且稀有模式的覆盖随容量增加改善有限。

Abstract: It remains unclear whether graph language models learn structural regularities or merely memorize training graphs; this cannot be resolved by current aggregate fidelity metrics alone. We develop a calibrated diagnostic protocol that combines frequent subgraph mining, a graph-level bootstrap baseline, and three-level frequency stratification to disentangle memorization from structural alignment. Using this framework, we show that graph language models can acquire structural regularities beyond memorization at scale, primarily in the high-frequency regime. This is supported by the following empirical evidence: On five TU benchmarks, LLaMA-style graph language models reach high subgraph-rank correlation, yet their alignment is matched or exceeded by the memorization bootstrap in most cases. At small scale, under our bootstrap diagnostic, fidelity is largely indistinguishable from verbatim recall. In contrast, at large scale with 3.75M graphs, verbatim memorization drops sharply while rank correlation remains near ceiling. Crucially, in a separate fixed-subsample analysis, frequent subgraph mining restricted to the novel-only subset closely tracks the corresponding all-generation Spearman correlation, providing evidence that the alignment is not driven solely by verbatim recall. Across all scales, high-frequency patterns are well reproduced, while rare patterns remain poorly covered, and this deficit narrows only marginally as capacity increases. We observe the same scale-dependent crossover under two distinct graph serializations (canonical DFS code and action sequences), providing evidence of robustness in our analysis.

</details>


### [260] [Cumulative-Goodness Free-Riding in Forward-Forward Networks: Real, Repairable, but Not Accuracy-Dominant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06240)
*Amirhossein Yousefiramandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了前向-前向（FF）训练中存在的“层搭便车”现象：在累积好度变体下，后续层会继承前面层已部分分离的任务，导致深层梯度随前面层累积的正间隔呈指数衰减。作者提出了三种局部补救方法——逐块、硬度门控和深度缩放——在不依赖反向传播梯度的情况下恢复当前层的分离度量。在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，这些方法显著改善了层分离统计量（深层提升4倍至45倍），但对非退化训练过程的精度改变不足1个百分点。Tiny ImageNet上的跨数据集验证显示了相同的定性差距。校准实验表明，架构和数据增强选择对最终精度的影响大于训练规则的修改。结论：累积搭便车是一种真实且可修复的优化病理，但在所研究的FF训练规则、架构和数据集上，它并非限制最终精度的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向-前向训练中，累积好度变体下后面层可能“搭便车”——前面层已部分完成分类任务，导致深层梯度衰减，层间学习不均衡。作者希望形式化这一现象并寻找局部补救方法。

Method: 1) 形式化分析：在softplus FF准则下，证明层d收到的类别判别梯度随前面块累积的正间隔呈指数衰减。2) 提出三种局部补救方法：逐块（per-block）、硬度门控（hardness-gated）和深度缩放（depth-scaled），旨在不依赖反向传播梯度的情况下恢复当前层的分离度量。3) 在CIFAR-10/100和Tiny ImageNet上进行实验验证。

Result: 三种局部补救方法在CIFAR-10/100上使深层层的分离统计量提升4倍至45倍，但精度变化不足1个百分点。Tiny ImageNet上观察到相同的定性模式。校准实验显示架构和数据增强对精度的影响大于训练规则修改。

Conclusion: 累积搭便车是真实存在且可修复的优化病理现象，但在当前研究的FF训练规则、架构和数据集上，它并非限制最终可达到精度的主导因素。

Abstract: Forward-Forward (FF) training allows each layer to learn from a local goodness criterion. In cumulative-goodness variants, however, later layers can inherit a task that earlier layers have already partially separated. We formalize this phenomenon as layer free-riding: under the softplus FF criterion, the class-discrimination gradient reaching block $d$ decays exponentially with the positive margin accumulated by preceding blocks. We then study three local remedies -- per-block, hardness-gated, and depth-scaled -- that recover current-layer separation measures without relying on backpropagated gradients. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, these remedies dramatically improve layer-separation statistics, with $4\times$--$45\times$ gains in deeper layers, while changing accuracy by less than one percentage point for non-degenerate training procedures. Tiny ImageNet provides a tougher cross-dataset check for our selected block-wise configuration and reveals the same qualitative gap between layer-health diagnostics and final accuracy. Calibration experiments further show that architecture and augmentation choices have a larger effect on final accuracy than the training-rule modifications studied here. Cumulative free-riding is therefore a real and repairable optimization pathology. Nonetheless, for the FF training rules, architectures, and datasets we study, it is not the dominant factor limiting achievable accuracy.

</details>


### [261] [Structure-Preserving Gaussian Processes Via Discrete Euler-Lagrange Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06246)
*Jan-Hendrik Ewering,Kathrin Flaßkamp,Niklas Wahlström,Thomas B. Schön,Thomas Seel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出拉格朗日高斯过程(LGPs)，通过离散受迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程实现概率且数据高效的动力学学习，在无外力时保持拉格朗日-达朗贝尔原理的几何结构，仅从离散位置快照即可学习动力学，实现稳定长期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力学学习方法通常需要速度或动量数据，且难以保证物理一致性，导致长期预测中的能量漂移问题。本文旨在仅利用位置测量数据，学习保持物理结构的不确定性量化模型。

Method: 基于离散受迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程和变分离散化方案，构建用于高斯过程条件化的线性算子，使学习过程隐式遵循拉格朗日-达朗贝尔原理的几何结构。

Result: 在多种合成和真实场景中验证了数据效率和泛化能力，包括真实软体机器人的迟滞现象。仅凭稀疏位置数据即可学习物理一致的动力学，实现稳定长期预测。

Conclusion: LGPs提供了一种仅需位置观测数据、保持物理一致性并具备不确定性量化能力的动力学学习方法，特别适用于运动捕捉和视觉伺服等实际应用场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Lagrangian Gaussian Processes (LGPs) for probabilistic and data-efficient learning of dynamics via discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations. Importantly, the geometric structure of the Lagrange-d'Alembert principle, which governs the motion of dynamical systems, is preserved by construction in the absence of external forces. This allows learning physically consistent models that overcome erroneous drift in the system's energy, thereby providing stable long-term predictions. At the core of our approach lie linear operators for Gaussian process conditioning, constructed from discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations and variational discretization schemes. Thereby and unlike prior work, the method enables learning dynamics from discrete position snapshots, i.e., without access to a system's velocities or momenta. This is particularly relevant for a large class of practical scenarios where only position measurements are available, for instance, in motion capture or visual servoing applications. We demonstrate the data-efficiency and generalization capabilities of the LGPs in various synthetic and real-world case studies, including a real-world soft robot with hysteresis. The experimental results underscore that the LGPs learn physically consistent dynamics with uncertainty quantification solely from sparse positional data and enable stable long-term predictions.

</details>


### [262] [Trade-off Functions for DP-SGD with Subsampling based on Random Shuffling: Tight Upper and Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06259)
*Marten van Dijk,Murat Bilgehan Ertan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在f-DP框架下对基于随机洗牌子采样的DP-SGD隐私权衡函数进行了紧致分析，推导出闭式界，并利用Berry-Esseen定理和推广的大数定律分别得到了单周期和多周期下的隐私保证，揭示了与泊松子采样的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有f-DP分析多针对泊松子采样，虽可机器计算但缺乏透明性；随机洗牌子采样缺乏紧致的闭式分析结果，且存在近期关于σ≤1/√(2lnM)的负面结果，需要澄清更广泛参数区间下的隐私保证。

Method: 在f-DP框架下对随机洗牌子采样的DP-SGD进行理论分析，使用Berry-Esseen定理推导紧致闭式界，并通过推广大数定律提出新证明技术以分析多epoch渐近行为。

Result: 给出了σ≥√(3/lnM)区间内的紧致闭式界；单周期时在δ=1/100、σ=1下M≈1.14×10^6、N≈1.14×10^7即可实现有意义的差分隐私；多周期时δ与E呈线性关系限制E=O(√M)；渐近分析表明若E=c_M²M且c_M→0，则组合后的权衡函数趋于理想猜测对角线1-a，δ仅O(√E)依赖。

Conclusion: 随机洗牌子采样在f-DP框架下可获得透明且紧致的隐私分析，与泊松子采样形成互补；明确收敛速率的刻画仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We derive a tight analysis of the trade-off function for Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) with subsampling based on random shuffling within the $f$-DP framework. Our analysis covers the regime $σ\geq \sqrt{3/\ln M}$, where $σ$ is the noise multiplier and $M$ is the number of rounds within a single epoch. Unlike $f$-DP analyses for Poisson subsampling, which yield non-closed implicit formulas that can be machine computed but are non-transparent, random shuffling admits a tight analysis yielding transparent and interpretable closed-form bounds. Our concrete bounds, derived via the Berry-Esseen theorem, are tight up to constant factors within the proof framework. We demonstrate worked parameter settings for a single epoch ($E=1$) with a corresponding trade-off function $\geq 1-a-δ$, that is, only $δ$ below the ideal random guessing diagonal $1-a$: For $δ= 1/100$ and $σ= 1$, roughly $M \approx 1.14\times 10^6$ rounds and $N \approx 1.14\times 10^7$ training samples suffice to achieve meaningful differential privacy. This is in contrast to recent negative results for the regime $σ\leq 1/\sqrt{2 \ln M}$. Our concrete bounds can be composed over multiple epochs leading to $δ$ having a linear in $E$ dependency, which restricts $E=O(\sqrt{M})$. To go beyond Berry--Esseen, we introduce a new proof technique based on a generalization of the law of large numbers that yields an asymptotic random guessing diagonal-limit result: if $E=c_M^2M$ with $c_M\to 0$, then the $E$-fold composed trade-off function satisfies $f^{\otimes E}(a)\to 1-a$ uniformly in $a\in[0,1]$ with $δ$ having only an $O(\sqrt{E})$ dependency. We compare this asymptotic regime with the corresponding Poisson subsampling asymptotic, and highlight the characterization of explicit convergence rates as an open question.

</details>


### [263] [Beyond Rigid Alignment: Graph Federated Learning via Dual Manifold Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06260)
*Wentao Yu,Bo Han,Jie Yang,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedGMC，一种通过双流形校准机制（语义流形和结构流形）解决图联邦学习中异质性问题的范式，在11个图数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图联邦学习方法通过刚性对齐模型参数或原型来处理异质性，这隐含地依赖全局线性假设，用一个全局一致的表征空间压缩了客户端的个性化表征空间，无法保留多样的局部图分布。

Method: 提出Federated Graph Manifold Calibration (FedGMC)，引入双流形校准机制：1) 语义异质性方面，服务端通过等距语义锚构建几何最优的语义流形，指导局部语义流形校准；2) 结构异质性方面，服务端通过构建全局结构模板构建全局结构流形，指导局部结构流形校准；3) 服务端通过聚合局部流形动态优化全局语义和结构流形。

Result: 在11个同质性和异质性图数据集上的大量实验表明，FedGMC有效平衡了全局共性和局部个性化，显著优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: FedGMC通过统一的流形视角同时处理语义和结构异质性，避免了刚性对齐的限制，在保护全局共性的同时最大化局部个性化表征空间。

Abstract: Graph Federated Learning (GFL) enables collaborative representation learning across distributed subgraphs while preserving privacy. However, heterogeneity remains a critical challenge, as subgraphs across clients typically differ significantly in both semantics and structures. Existing methods address heterogeneity by enforcing the rigid alignment of model parameters or prototypes between clients and the server. However, these alignments implicitly rely on a restrictive global linearity assumption that summarizes local data distributions using a single and globally consistent representation space. This severely compresses the personalized representation space of clients and fails to preserve diverse local graph distributions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Federated Graph Manifold Calibration (FedGMC), a novel paradigm that tackles semantic heterogeneity and structural heterogeneity from a unified manifold perspective. Instead of enforcing rigid alignment, FedGMC introduces a dual manifold calibration mechanism that preserves global commonalities while maximizing the personalized representation space of local clients. Specifically, for semantic heterogeneity, the server constructs a geometrically optimal semantic manifold via equidistant semantic anchors, so as to guide the calibration of local semantic manifolds. For structural heterogeneity, the server constructs a global structural manifold by building global structural templates, so as to guide the calibration of local structural manifolds. Finally, the server dynamically refines both global semantic manifolds and structural manifolds by aggregating local manifolds. Extensive experiments on eleven homophilic and heterophilic graphs demonstrate that FedGMC effectively balances global commonality and local personalization, thereby significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [264] [Inference-Time Refinement Closes the Synthetic-Real Gap in Tabular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06261)
*Eugenio Lomurno,Filippo Balzarini,Francesco Benelle,Francesca Pia Panaccione,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TARDIS框架，通过对预训练扩散模型进行推理时精炼（无需重新训练），在表格数据生成上超越真实数据效用，中位下游任务提升+8.6%。核心是双向Chamfer精炼（BCR）模式，在分数层面和离散层面同时最小化合成与真实样本间的对称Chamfer泛函。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散表格生成器在训练时通过架构改进、缩放和重新训练来缩小合成-真实差距，但推理时精炼（保持预训练骨干参数不变、仅优化输出）这一路径尚未被探索。

Method: TARDIS框架包含：(1) 基于Tree-structured Parzen Estimator的搜索，在反向扩散过程中调整分数级引导；(2) 内部网格搜索后验样本选择器和可选的软标签蒸馏；(3) 核心模式BCR，对称Chamfer泛函通过分数级梯度（连续）和批量排序后生成（离散）同时最小化。

Result: 在15个基准上，中位下游任务提升+8.6%（95% CI [+3.3, +16.4], p=0.016, 11/15严格胜出）；在所有15个数据集上优于TabDiff骨干（平均+12.9%, p<10^-4）；流形保真度、多样性和样本级隐私与骨干持平；单消费级GPU上仅需1-80分钟。

Conclusion: 推理时精炼预训练表格扩散骨干可以达到并超越真实数据效用，BCR是主导的精炼模式，为表格数据合成提供了一条无需重新训练的高效路径。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generators set the current state of the art for synthetic tabular data. These methods approach but rarely exceed real-data utility, and closing this synthetic-real gap has so far been pursued exclusively at training time, via architectural advances, scaling, and retraining of monolithic generators. The inference-time alternative, i.e., refining the outputs of a pre-trained backbone with parameters left untouched, has remained largely unexplored for tabular synthesis. We introduce TARDIS (Tabular generation through Refinement, Distillation, and Inference-time Sampling), an inference-time refinement framework that operates on a frozen pre-trained backbone, configured per dataset by a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator search over score-level guidance during reverse diffusion, with each trial's objective set by an inner grid search over post-hoc sample selectors and an optional soft-label distillation step. The search space encodes a single mathematical pattern we name Bidirectional Chamfer Refinement (BCR): the symmetric Chamfer functional between synthetic and real samples is minimized both continuously, via a score-level gradient, and discretely, via batch-ranking post-generation. The per-dataset search recovers BCR-aligned configurations on most datasets, evidence for BCR as the dominant refinement pattern. Across 15 binary, multiclass, and regression benchmarks TARDIS achieves a median +8.6% downstream-task improvement over models trained on real data (95% CI [+3.3, +16.4], Wilcoxon p=0.016, 11/15 strict wins) and improves over the TabDiff backbone on all 15 datasets (mean +12.9%, p<10^-4), matching the backbone on manifold fidelity, diversity, and sample-level privacy. Inference-time refinement of a pre-trained tabular diffusion backbone reaches and exceeds real-data utility in 1 to 80 minutes on a single consumer-grade GPU.

</details>


### [265] [Can Attribution Predict Risk? From Multi-View Attribution to Planning Risk Signals in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06264)
*Le Yang,Ruoyu Chen,Haijun Liu,Jiawei Liang,ShangQuan Sun,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向端到端自动驾驶规划的分层归因框架，通过粗到细的区域归因策略从六视角输入中定位关键视觉证据，并提取三种归因统计量（熵、空间方差、基尼系数）作为风险预测信号，实验表明该方法能有效关联轨迹误差并检测碰撞风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶模型虽然提升了系统集成度，但其决策过程不透明且风险难以定位。已有的辅助监控或文本解释方法与规划过程解耦，无法揭示轨迹生成背后的视觉证据。而归因方法虽是一种直接方案，但规划任务与图像分类不同，需要同时捕获关键视角和区域及其对多步连续轨迹输出的影响，且归因图能否支持风险识别尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出分层归因框架：1）以与原始轨迹的L2一致性为优化目标，设计粗到细的区域归因策略，先在六视角全景中搜索候选区域，再在区域内细化归因；2）提取三类归因统计量：归因熵（衡量注意力集中度）、视角内空间方差（衡量视角内归因分散程度）、跨视角基尼系数（衡量六视角间归因分布的不均匀性），作为规划风险的预测信号。

Result: 在BridgeAD、UniAD和GenAD三个模型上进行实验，归因统计量与轨迹误差的Spearman相关系数达到0.30±0.07，碰撞检测的AUROC达到0.77±0.04。该信号在未见场景中泛化良好且性能下降可忽略，在不同归因基线下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 提出的分层归因框架能有效揭示端到端规划模型决策的视觉证据，且归因统计量可作为可靠的规划风险预测信号，具有良好的泛化性和稳定性。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models generate future trajectories from multi-view inputs, improving system integration but introducing opaque decisions and hard-to-localize risks. Existing methods either rely on auxiliary monitoring models or generate textual explanations, but are decoupled from the planning process and fail to reveal the visual evidence underlying trajectory generation. While attribution offers a direct alternative, planning differs from image classification by taking six-view camera images as input and predicting continuous multi-step trajectories, requiring attribution to capture both critical views and regions and their influence on outputs. Moreover, whether attribution maps can support risk identification remains underexplored. To address this, we propose a hierarchical attribution framework for end-to-end planning. Specifically, using L2 consistency with the original trajectory as the objective, we design a coarse-to-fine region attribution strategy that searches candidate regions across the full six-view input and refines attribution within them. We further extract three attribution statistics as predictive signals for planning risk, including attribution entropy to measure how concentrated the planner's reliance is over the joint visual space, within-camera spatial variance to characterize how spread out the attribution is within each view, and cross-camera Gini coefficient to quantify how unevenly attribution is distributed across the six cameras. Experiments on BridgeAD, UniAD, and GenAD show that these statistics correlate with planning risk, achieving Spearman correlations of $0.30 \pm 0.07$ with trajectory error and AUROC of $0.77 \pm 0.04$ for collision detection. The signal generalizes to held-out scenes with negligible degradation and remains stable under an alternative attribution baseline.

</details>


### [266] [A Flow Matching Algorithm for Many-Shot Adaptation to Unseen Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06272)
*Tyler Ingebrand,Ruihan Zhao,Kushagra Gupta,David Fridovich-Keil,Sandeep P. Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出函数投影流匹配（FP-FM），通过在训练分布的速度场空间学习基函数，并利用最小二乘投影适应新分布，无需额外训练即可高效生成目标分布样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成模型在自然语言条件生成上取得了显著成功，但从示例数据点进行模型适应仍然是一个探索不足且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: FP-FM学习一组基函数来张成训练分布对应的速度场空间，通过计算最小二乘投影将新分布适应到该基函数上；同时引入多种变体（如时间依赖系数）以在表达能力和计算量之间进行权衡。

Result: 在合成数据和图像数据集上，FP-FM相对于基线方法在精度和召回率上均有显著提升，尤其是在未见过的分布上表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: FP-FM能够高效地从多样化目标分布中生成样本，无需推理时的额外训练，同时保持高精度和召回率。

Abstract: While generative modeling has achieved remarkable success on tasks like natural language-conditioned image generation, enabling model adaptation from example data points remains a relatively underexplored and challenging problem. To this end, we propose Function Projection for Flow Matching (FP-FM), an algorithm that directly conditions generation on samples from the target distribution. FP-FM learns basis functions to span the velocity fields corresponding to a set of training distributions, and adapts to new distributions by computing a simple least-squares projection onto this basis. This enables efficient generation of samples from diverse target distributions without additional training at inference time. We further introduce multiple variants of FP-FM that provide a trade-off in expressivity and compute by enriching the coefficient calculation, e.g., by making the coefficients dependent on time. FP-FM achieves greatly improved precision and recall relative to baselines across synthetic and image-based datasets, with especially strong gains on unseen distributions.

</details>


### [267] [PACE: Prune-And-Compress Ensemble Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06278)
*Fabian Akkerman,Julien Ferry,Théo Guyard,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACE是一种两阶段框架，通过先主动生成新学习器增强集成多样性，再剪枝冗余学习器，在保证忠实度的前提下优于现有剪枝和压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成模型虽性能优越，但大量弱学习器聚合导致部署困难、可解释性差和鲁棒性验证受阻。现有剪枝和压缩方法各有不足。

Method: 提出PACE框架，采用两阶段策略：第一阶段通过理论驱动的过程主动生成新学习器以增强集成多样性；第二阶段对增强后的集成进行剪枝。两个阶段均可精细控制对原始集成的忠实度。

Result: 实验表明PACE在性能上优于现有剪枝和压缩方法，同时提供有原则的忠实度保证控制。

Conclusion: PACE通过交织生成与剪枝的两阶段策略，在保证忠实度的前提下有效提升了集成模型的压缩效果。

Abstract: Ensemble models achieve state-of-the-art performance on prediction tasks, but usually require aggregating a large number of weak learners. This can hinder deployment, interpretability, and downstream tasks such as robustness verification. Remedies to this issue fall into two main camps: pruning, which discards redundant learners, and compression, which generates new ones from scratch. We introduce PACE, a framework that interleaves these paradigms in a two-phase strategy. First, new learners are actively generated via a theoretically grounded procedure to enhance the diversity of the initial ensemble. When no more relevant learners can be found, a second phase of pruning is performed on this enriched ensemble. During both operations, PACE allows fine control on the faithfulness to the original ensemble. Experiments show that our method outperforms prior pruning and compression methods while offering principled control of faithfulness guarantees.

</details>


### [268] [INEUS: Iterative Neural Solver for High-Dimensional PIDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06281)
*Jean-Loup Dupret,Davide Gallon,Patrick Cheridito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INEUS是一种无网格迭代神经求解器，通过单跳采样替代非局部跳积分显式求值，将偏积分微分方程求解转化为递归回归问题，在保持PINNs全局求解能力的同时更高效处理非局部项，适用于高维线性和非线性PDE/PIDE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如PINNs）在处理PIDE中的非局部跳积分项时计算代价高昂，且对完整PIDE残差进行微分在计算上非常昂贵，需要一种更高效的方法来求解高维PDE和PIDE。

Method: 提出INEUS方法，核心创新包括：(1) 用单跳采样替代显式非局部跳积分求值；(2) 将PIDE求解转化为序列递归回归问题；(3) 采用无网格方式学习整个时空域上的全局解。

Result: 对于线性PIDE，给出了基于压缩映射的收敛性证明。数值实验表明，INEUS在各种高维线性和非线性示例中都能提供准确且可扩展的求解结果。

Conclusion: INEUS在保持PINNs全局求解能力的同时，更高效地处理非局部项，避免了计算昂贵的全PIDE残差微分，特别适合高维PDE和PIDE问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce INEUS, a meshfree iterative neural solver for partial integro-differential equations (PIDEs). The method replaces the explicit evaluation of nonlocal jump integrals with single-jump sampling and reformulates PIDE solving as a sequence of recursive regression problems. Like Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), INEUS learns global solutions over the entire space-time domain, yet it offers a more efficient treatment of nonlocal terms and avoids the computationally expensive differentiation of full PIDE residuals. These features make INEUS particularly well suited for high-dimensional PDEs and PIDEs. Supported by a contraction-based convergence proof for linear PIDEs, our numerical experiments show that INEUS delivers accurate and scalable solutions for various high-dimensional linear and nonlinear examples.

</details>


### [269] [Attributions All the Way Down? The Metagame of Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06295)
*Hubert Baniecki,Przemyslaw Biecek,Fabian Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“元博弈”（metagame）概念框架，通过将解释方法本身视为合作博弈并计算其Shapley值，量化模型解释的二阶交互效应（即特征j对特征i归因的影响，称为元归因）。理论上证明归因可层次分解为元归因，并将其建立为现有交互指标的定向扩展。实验在指令微调语言模型、视觉-语言编码器及多模态扩散变压器中展示其效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型解释方法多关注一阶归因（单个特征对预测的贡献），但缺乏对特征间二阶交互效应（即一个特征如何影响另一个特征的归因）的系统性量化框架。

Method: 将归因方法本身视为合作博弈，通过计算其Shapley值来度量特征j对特征i归因的定向影响（元归因），实现归因的层次分解并建立与现有交互指标的联系。

Result: 理论上证明了归因可分解为元归因，并作为定向扩展的交互指标；实证上在指令微调语言模型、视觉-语言编码器和多模态扩散变压器中展示了有效性。

Conclusion: 元博弈框架提供了一种量化模型解释中二阶交互效应的通用方法，扩展了现有归因交互分析的维度，适用于多种可解释性应用场景。

Abstract: We introduce the metagame, a conceptual framework for quantifying second-order interaction effects of model explanations. For any first-order attribution $φ(f)$ explaining a model $f$, we measure the directional influence of feature $j$ on the attribution of feature $i$, denoted as meta-attribution $\varphi_{j \to i}(f)$, by treating the attribution method itself as a cooperative game and computing its Shapley value. Theoretically, we prove that attributions hierarchically decompose into meta-attributions, and establish these as directional extensions of existing interaction indices. Empirically, we demonstrate that the metagame delivers insights across diverse interpretability applications: (i) quantifying token interactions in instruction-tuned language models, (ii) explaining cross-modal similarity in vision-language encoders, and (iii) interpreting text-to-image concepts in multimodal diffusion transformers.

</details>


### [270] [Molecules Meet Language: Confound-Aware Representation Learning and Chemical Property Steering in Transformer-VAE Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06303)
*Zakaria Elabid,Jan Andrzejewski,Bartosz Brzoza,Attila Cangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在无监督自回归Transformer-VAE（SELFIES分子表示）中研究潜在空间化学意义的可操纵性，发现SELFIES长度、分支/环标记、标记熵等序列级捷径被强烈编码。通过残差化、混淆方向对齐分析和解码分子遍历的混淆感知评估，验证了cLogP等6种性质存在稳健单调操纵，而其他性质更适合局部潜在梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子生成模型常假设潜在空间具有有意义的化学几何结构，但性质可预测性可能源于序列级捷径而非真实的化学组织。现有研究缺乏对分子表示（如SELFIES）中序列伪影与真实化学信号之间混淆的系统性分离方法。

Method: 使用无监督自回归Transformer-VAE训练SELFIES分子；冻结模型后对RDKit描述符拟合线性探针，将探针权重作为候选全局操纵方向；引入基于残差化、混淆方向对齐分析和解码分子遍历的混淆感知评估框架，分离化学信号与SELFIES伪影。

Result: SELFIES长度、分支标记、环标记和标记熵在潜空间中强烈编码；在控制混淆后，cLogP、FractionCSP3、HeavyAtomCount、TPSA、BertzCT和HBA存在稳健单调操纵；非线性探针表明部分性质存在稳定全局方向，另一些更适合局部潜在梯度。

Conclusion: 化学有意义的操纵可以在纠缠的分子潜在空间中实现，但必须通过解码分子验证并控制表示层面的混淆因素。

Abstract: Molecular generative models often assume meaningful latent geometry, but apparent property predictability can reflect sequence-level shortcuts rather than chemical organization. We study this issue in an unsupervised autoregressive Transformer-VAE trained on SELFIES. After training, we freeze the model, fit linear probes to RDKit descriptors, and use the probe weights as candidate global steering directions. To separate chemical signal from SELFIES artifacts, we introduce a confound-aware evaluation based on residualization, confound-direction alignment analysis, and decoded-molecule traversal. This is necessary because SELFIES length, branch tokens, ring tokens, and token entropy are strongly encoded in the latent space. Under this confound-aware evaluation, we find robust monotonic steering for cLogP, FractionCSP3, HeavyAtomCount, TPSA, BertzCT, and HBA. Nonlinear probes further show that some properties admit stable global directions, while others are better described by local latent gradients. Overall, our results show that chemically meaningful steering can emerge in entangled molecular latent spaces, but only when validated through decoded molecules and controlled for representation-level confounds.

</details>


### [271] [When Does $\ell_2$-Boosting Overfit Benignly? High-Dimensional Risk Asymptotics and the $\ell_1$ Implicit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06314)
*Ye Su,Jian Li,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了贪婪集成方法（ℓ₂-Boosting）在高维统计中的良性过拟合行为。不同于ℓ₂几何中熟知的线性率，在ℓ₁隐式偏差下，贪婪选择将噪声局域化入稀疏活跃集，导致过拟合方差以对数率 Θ(σ²/log(p/n)) 衰减。对于尖峰-各向同性设计，风险仅在尾部维度远大于样本量时趋于零，且同样为对数率。论文进一步通过分析非光滑次微分动力学，提出了无需调参的早停规则，在ℓ₁-路径有界条件下恢复了Lasso基本不等式并达到极小化最优经验预测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ℓ₂几何中的良性过拟合已被充分刻画，但ℓ₁隐式偏差下的过拟合行为因坐标选择阈值的非线性耦合而难以分析。现有谱分解工具在此失效，需要新的理论工具来解析贪婪集成方法的非光滑插值行为。

Method: 将凸高斯极小化定理与双边截断高斯矩的渐近展开相结合，解析求解非光滑ℓ₁插值问题。对纯噪声模型和各向同性尖峰设计分别推导风险收敛率，并利用次微分动力学分析Boosting流的早停机制。

Result: 纯噪声下，良性过拟合以对数率失败（Θ(σ²/log(p/n))）；尖峰-各向同性设计中，仅在尾部维度 r₂ ≫ n 时风险趋于零，且仍为对数率，慢于ℓ₂几何中的线性衰减。早停规则可在ℓ₁-路径有界条件下达到极小化最优率。

Conclusion: ℓ₁隐式偏差下的贪婪选择会导致噪声局域化，使得过拟合率从线性降为对数，这揭示了不同几何归纳偏置对高维插值行为本质不同的影响。早停策略可缓解该问题并恢复最优收敛率。

Abstract: Benign overfitting is well-characterized in $\ell_2$ geometries, but its behavior under the $\ell_1$ implicit bias of greedy ensembles remains challenging. The analytical barrier stems from the non-linear coupling of coordinate selection thresholds, which invalidates standard spectral resolvent tools. To isolate this algorithmic bias, we characterize the high-dimensional risk of continuous-time $\ell_2$-Boosting over $p$ features and $n$ samples. By coupling the Convex Gaussian Minimax Theorem with delicate asymptotic expansions of double-sided truncated Gaussian moments, we analytically resolve the non-smooth $\ell_1$ interpolant. Under an isotropic pure-noise model, we prove that benign overfitting fails at the linear rate: greedy selection localizes noise into sparse active sets, and the excess variance decays at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(p/n))$ for noise variance $σ^2$. We remark that while this localization mechanism should persist in the presence of signals, the exact signal-noise decomposition remains an open problem. For spiked-isotropic designs with $k^*$ head eigenvalues and $r_2 = p - k^*$ tail dimensions, the risk converges to zero when $r_{2} \gg n$, but only at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(r_2/n))$, which is slower than the linear decay observed in $\ell_2$ geometries. To avoid this slow convergence, we analyze the non-smooth subdifferential dynamics of the boosting flow. This yields a tuning-free early stopping rule that, under a bounded $\ell_1$-path condition, recovers the Lasso basic inequality and attains the minimax-optimal empirical prediction rate for $\ell_1$-bounded signals.

</details>


### [272] [Pro-KLShampoo: Projected KL-Shampoo with Whitening Recovered by Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06316)
*Ruotong Sun,Ermin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现KL-Shampoo的Kronecker预条件子具有"尖峰-平坦"特征谱结构，据此提出Pro-KLShampoo方法：在r维子空间保留完整谱结构，剩余方向共享单一特征值并应用正交化。该方法在多个规模上优于KL-Shampoo，降低内存和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kronecker分解预条件与梯度动量正交化两条技术路线长期独立发展，缺乏统一理解。作者观察到KL-Shampoo的预条件子呈现尖峰-平坦谱结构，希望利用此结构设计更高效的优化器。

Method: 提出Pro-KLShampoo：将KL-Shampoo的一个Kronecker因子参数化为"尖峰-平坦"形式——在追踪的r维子空间保留完整谱结构，剩余n-r个方向共享单一特征值并应用正交化。理论证明了该正交化操作等价于完整KL-Shampoo预条件子的代数形式。

Result: 在GPT-2 124M/350M、LLaMA 134M/450M四个预训练规模上，Pro-KLShampoo在所有测试的子空间秩上均优于KL-Shampoo，在验证损失、峰值每GPU内存和达到各损失水平的墙钟时间三个指标上全面领先。

Conclusion: 利用预条件子的尖峰-平坦谱结构，通过子空间降维与正交化结合，可以同时保持KL-Shampoo的优化性能并降低计算与内存开销，为两种优化范式的融合提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Optimizers that exploit the matrix structure of gradients are central to modern LLM pre-training, with two distinct frontiers: explicit Kronecker-factored preconditioning -- most recently KL-Shampoo, which estimates the preconditioner via KL divergence minimization -- and orthogonalization of the gradient momentum, exemplified by Muon and analyzed as steepest descent under the spectral norm. The two routes are typically developed in isolation. We make a structural observation about KL-Shampoo's Kronecker preconditioners: their eigenvalue spectra exhibit a \emph{spike-and-flat} shape -- a few dominant eigenvalues followed by an approximately uniform tail -- across layers and training stages, holding exactly under a rank-$ρ$ signal-plus-noise gradient model. We exploit this structure by restricting one of KL-Shampoo's Kronecker factors to a parametric family aligned with the spike-and-flat shape: full spectral structure on a tracked $r$-dimensional subspace, single shared eigenvalue across the remaining $n-r$ directions. On these directions, we apply orthogonalization. An identity shows that this orthogonalization recovers the algebraic form of full KL-Shampoo's preconditioner. On four pre-training scales (GPT-2 124M / 350M, LLaMA 134M / 450M), Pro-KLShampoo consistently outperforms KL-Shampoo at every subspace rank we test in validation loss, peak per-GPU memory, and wallclock time to reach each loss level.

</details>


### [273] [LINC: Decoupling Local Consequence Scoring from Hidden Matching in Constructive Neural Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06332)
*Shaofeng Qin,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LINC（局部推理通过归一化比较）解码器架构，显式计算决策中的局部后果（如行驶、等待、松弛、容量变化），通过共享线性评分器比较归一化相对后果，并用可行集摘要调节解码器上下文，显著提升了CVRPTW、CVRP和TSP的求解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有构造式神经路由求解器通常通过匹配解码器上下文与候选嵌入来评分下一步动作，隐藏了行驶、等待、松弛、容量变化等确定性单步后果，导致隐藏状态需要重新发现转移算术，效率低下。

Method: 提出LINC解码器架构：1) 显式计算每一步的局部后果（旅行、等待、松弛、容量变化等）；2) 根据决策角色使用这些后果——中心化相对后果通过共享线性局部评分器进行比较；3) 可行集摘要用于调节解码器上下文；4) 保留全局匹配机制。

Result: 在CVRPTW上，将PolyNet在Solomon和Homberger基准上的差距从13.83%/38.15%降低到7.26%/14.71%；在TSP和CVRP上也改进了外部基准差距。

Conclusion: LINC通过显式建模局部过渡后果，改善了神经路由求解器在多种约束路由问题上的性能，为处理约束路由问题提供了有效的解码器设计范式。

Abstract: Constructive neural routing solvers usually score the next action by matching a decoder context to candidate embeddings, hiding deterministic one-step consequences such as travel, waiting, slack, and capacity changes. We propose LINC (Local Inference via Normed Comparison), a decoder-side candidate decision architecture that computes these consequences explicitly. LINC uses them according to their decision role: centered relative consequences are compared by a shared linear local scorer, while feasible-set summaries modulate the decoder context. This preserves standard global matching and relieves the hidden state from rediscovering transition arithmetic. The Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVRPTW) serves as the main constrained-routing stress test; the same interface extends to the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP) and Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP). In particular, for CVRPTW, LINC reduces PolyNet's Solomon/Homberger gaps from 13.83\%/38.15\% to 7.26\%/14.71\%; for TSP and CVRP, it also improves external-benchmark gaps.

</details>


### [274] [Eliciting associations between clinical variables from LLMs via comparison questions across populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06335)
*Fabian Kabus,Kian Kordtomeikel,Thomas Brox,Heinz Wiendl,Daiana Stolz,Harald Binder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于结构化比较三元组问题的间接查询方法，结合统计模型和环境偏移策略，从LLM中恢复患者特征间的相关性和因果关系，并在COPD和MS两个临床领域验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的训练数据包含大量生物医学文献，反映不同患者群体的数据，但直接查询LLM获取相关性和因果信息存在偏差风险，因此需要一种间接、可靠的途径来恢复患者特征间的关联结构，为医疗决策提供支持。

Method: 提出基于患者比较三元组问题的结构化查询方法，结合统计模型估计LLM中的相关性（无需访问激活或内部状态）。通过改变提示中的环境信息（环境偏移）获得不同子群体的相关性估计，再利用不变因果预测（ICP）方法获取保守的候选因果父节点链接。

Result: 在COPD和MS两个临床领域，不同提示环境下获得的相关性估计平滑、稳定且具有临床可解释性，同时在统计上存在显著差异，支持下游不变性检验；ICP方法产生了少量候选不变父节点链接。

Conclusion: 间接三元组比较方法能从LLM中恢复有意义的关联结构，提供了一条从隐式相关到因果陈述的谨慎路径，且与LLM的回答模式一致。

Abstract: The training data of large language models (LLMs) comprises a wide range of biomedical literature, reflecting data from many different patient populations. We investigate how it might be possible to recover information on correlation and causal links between patient characteristics, as a key building block for medical decision making. To avoid the pitfalls of direct elicitation, we propose an approach based on structured comparison questions, specifically patient comparison triplet questions. This is combined with a statistical model for the LLM representation that provides estimates of correlations without access to activations or model internals. Intuitively, we consider how similarity decisions of LLMs based on a first variable are affected by providing information on a second variable for one of the patients being assessed. We then induce prompt-level environment shifts to obtain correlation estimates for different subpopulations, which enables an invariant causal prediction (ICP) approach to obtain conservative candidate parent links. We demonstrate the method in two clinical domains, chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD) and multiple sclerosis (MS). Across prompted environments, the elicited correlations are smooth, stable, and clinically interpretable, yet vary in a statistically significant way that supports downstream invariance testing, such that ICP provides a small set of candidate invariant parent links. These results show that indirect elicitation via triplet comparisons can recover meaningful association structure from LLMs and offer a cautious route from implicit correlations to causal statements that are congruent with LLM answering patterns.

</details>


### [275] [Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Modelling with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06355)
*Ignacio Peis,Pablo M. Olmos,Jes Frellsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了顺序无关自回归模型（Order-Agnostic AR Models），将其置于缺失数据视角下解读：发现其在完全数据上的标准训练隐式执行了完全随机缺失（MCAR）机制下的插补，具备高缺失率下的鲁棒性；提出了首个能在一般缺失机制下直接基于不完整数据进行训练的原理性框架；并利用其摊销条件密度估计实现主动信息获取（顺序选择最有信息量的缺失变量）。在多个真实基准上，所提出的MO-ARM模型持续优于已有插补基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序无关自回归模型在深度生成建模中表现优异，但它们在缺失数据场景下的应用尚未被充分探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 重新解释标准训练过程，证明其在MCAR机制下隐式进行插补；2) 提出首个直接在不完整数据上训练的原理性框架，支持一般缺失机制；3) 利用摊销条件密度估计进行主动信息获取，选择最信息量的缺失变量。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准上，MO-ARM模型持续优于已建立的插补基线方法。

Conclusion: 顺序无关自回归模型通过缺失数据视角的重新阐释，能够在高缺失率下鲁棒插补，且所提框架可推广到一般缺失机制和主动信息获取任务。

Abstract: Order-Agnostic autoregressive models have demonstrated strong performance in deep generative modeling, yet their use in settings with incomplete data remains largely unexplored. In this work, we reinterpret them through the lens of missing data. First, we show that their standard training procedure on fully observed data implicitly performs imputation under a missing completely at random mechanism, resulting in robust out-of-sample imputation performance in settings with high missingness. Second, we introduce the first principled framework for training them directly on incomplete datasets under general missingness mechanisms. Third, we leverage their amortized conditional density estimation to perform active information acquisition, i.e., sequentially selecting the most informative missing variables for downstream prediction or inference. Across a suite of real-world benchmarks, our Missingness-Aware Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Model (MO-ARM) consistently outperforms established imputation baselines.

</details>


### [276] [Preliminary Insights in Chronos Frequency Data Understanding and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06361)
*Alessandro Pagani,Marco Cominelli,Liying Han,Gaofeng Dong,Sergio Benini,Francesco Gringoli,Mattia Savardi,Mani B. Srivastava,Trevor Bihl,Erik P. Blasch,Daniel O. Brigham,Kara Combs,Lance M. Kaplan,Federico Cerutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文初步分析了Chronos基础模型处理和内表示频域信息的能力，通过轻量级在线最小描述长度探针测试解码器架构，发现模型能够捕获频率信息但存在表示质量退化的频率区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管处理时间序列数据的基础模型被广泛采用，但这类模型编码基本信号属性的程度尚未得到充分表征，存在可解释性缺口。

Method: 以离散正弦信号为输入，在受控条件下使用轻量级在线最小描述长度探针分析Chronos解码器架构的内部表示，测试频率信息的存在性与可分性。

Result: 模型内部表示能够捕获频域内容，但不同频率区间存在表示质量差异，部分区间可能出现退化或需要特别处理。

Conclusion: 为Chronos用户在信号处理和信息融合场景中提供实践指导，并有助于推进时序基础模型可解释性与评估的持续研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a preliminary analysis of the ability of Chronos foundation model to process and internally represent frequency domain information. Foundation models that process time-series data offer practitioners a unified architecture capable of learning generic temporal representations across diverse tasks and domains, reducing the need for task-specific feature engineering and enabling transfer across signal modalities. Despite their growing adoption, the extent to which such models encode fundamental signal properties remains insufficiently characterised. We address this gap by analysing Chronos under controlled conditions, starting from the simplest class of signals: discrete sinusoids generated at fixed frequencies. Using lightweight online minimum description length probes applied to the decoder architecture, we test for the presence and separability of frequency information in the model's internal representations. The results provide insight into how frequential content is captured across the frequency spectrum and highlight regimes in which representation quality may degrade or require particular care. These findings offer practical guidance for users of Chronos in signal processing and information fusion contexts, and contribute to ongoing efforts to improve the interpretability and evaluation of foundation models for temporal data.

</details>


### [277] [Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06364)
*Xin Peng,Ang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AuxPath-FM框架，通过引入任意分布的辅助变量η来泛化条件流匹配，允许使用多种先验分布设计概率路径，并支持标签引导生成等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件流匹配方法将辅助组件限制为高斯噪声，缺乏灵活性。作者希望打破这一限制，允许辅助变量服从任意分布，从而设计更多样化的概率路径。

Method: 在条件流匹配中引入辅助变量η（服从任意分布），定义轨迹形式为Xt = a(t)X1 + b(t)X0 + c(t)η，并证明该构造保持连续性方程且训练目标与边际形式一致。

Result: 理论上证明了该框架的连续性方程成立和训练目标的一致性；实验上展示了使用高斯、均匀、拉普拉斯和离散Rademacher分布等多种先验设计的概率路径，以及标签引导生成等特殊任务。

Conclusion: AuxPath-FM为概率路径设计提供了原则性且通用的理论基础，兼具理论泛化性和实际灵活性，适用于多种生成建模任务。

Abstract: We introduce a new generative modeling framework, \textbf{Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths (AuxPath-FM)}, which generalizes conditional flow matching by incorporating an auxiliary variable drawn from an arbitrary distribution into the probability path. Unlike prior methods that restrict auxiliary components to Gaussian noise, AuxPath-FM allows the variable $η$ to follow any distribution, producing trajectories of the form $X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η$. We theoretically demonstrate that this construction preserves the continuity equation and maintains a training objective consistent with the marginal formulation. This flexibility enables the design of diverse probability paths using various priors, including Gaussian, Uniform, Laplace, and discrete Rademacher distributions, each offering unique geometric properties for generative flows. Furthermore, our framework allows for specialized tasks such as label-guided generation by encoding structured semantic information into the auxiliary distribution. Overall, AuxPath-FM provides a principled and general foundation for probability path design, offering both theoretical generality and practical flexibility for diverse generative modeling tasks.

</details>


### [278] [A Unified Pair-GRPO Family: From Implicit to Explicit Preference Constraints for Stable and General RL Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06375)
*Hao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pair-GRPO系列方法（Soft-Pair-GRPO和Hard-Pair-GRPO），通过统一理论框架解决RLHF中策略更新不稳定、梯度方向模糊等问题，在LLM对齐和连续控制任务上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于成对偏好学习的RLHF范式存在策略更新不稳定、梯度方向不明确、可解释性差、梯度方差高等系统性缺陷，亟需一个统一的理论框架来系统性解决这些问题。

Method: 首先提出Soft-Pair-GRPO，将GRPO的组归一化标量奖励替换为二值成对偏好奖励，保留clipped surrogate和KL正则化结构，并证明其梯度与GRPO梯度呈正标量倍数关系。在此基础上提出Hard-Pair-GRPO，引入显式局部概率约束和约束KL-fitting优化以抑制梯度噪声和全局策略漂移。

Result: 在HH-RLHF、UltraFeedback和HalfCheetah-v4上的实验表明，Pair-GRPO系列在对齐质量、人类偏好胜率、训练稳定性和泛化能力上持续优于SOTA基线。消融实验验证了各核心组件的关键贡献。

Conclusion: Pair-GRPO系列通过理论保证（单调策略改进、确定性梯度方向、梯度方差降低、动态步长收敛）和经验验证，为偏好强化学习提供了稳定、可解释且高效的新范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) alignment via reinforcement learning from human preferences (RLHF) suffers from unstable policy updates, ambiguous gradient directions, poor interpretability, and high gradient variance in mainstream pairwise preference learning paradigms. To systematically address these limitations, we establish a unified theoretical framework for preference-based RL optimization centered on the Pair-GRPO family, comprising two tightly coupled variants: Soft-Pair-GRPO and Hard-Pair-GRPO. Soft-Pair-GRPO is a minimal modification of Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) that replaces group-normalized scalar rewards with binary pairwise preference rewards, retaining GRPO's clipped surrogate and KL-regularized structure. We prove a critical gradient equivalence theorem: under first-order Taylor expansion around the current policy, Soft-Pair-GRPO's gradient is a positive scalar multiple of standard GRPO's gradient, explaining its empirical stability despite discarding continuous reward magnitudes. Building on this foundation, we propose Hard-Pair-GRPO, an advanced variant introducing explicit local probability constraints and constrained KL-fitting optimization to further suppress gradient noise and global policy drift. We provide comprehensive theoretical guarantees for both variants--including monotonic policy improvement, deterministic gradient direction, gradient-variance reduction, and dynamic step-size convergence. Extensive experiments on standard LLM alignment benchmarks (HH-RLHF,UltraFeedback) and the MuJoCo continuous control task HalfCheetah-v4 demonstrate that our Pair-GRPO family consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in alignment quality, human preference win rate, training stability, and generalization to general reinforcement learning. Ablation studies validate the critical contributions of each core component.

</details>


### [279] [Data-Driven Covariate Selection for Nonparametric and Cycle-Agnostic Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06385)
*Ana Leticia Garcez Vicente,Gijs van Seeventer,Saber Salehkaleybar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一种基于条件独立性的局部协变量选择方法在循环因果模型中依然有效，通过σ-无环化保持条件独立不变性，统一了循环与无环场景下的因果效应估计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果效应估计方法通常假设无环性或依赖全局因果结构学习，在存在潜在混淆和反馈循环的现实场景中适用性受限且计算效率低。需要一种局部、数据驱动的协变量选择方法，并验证其在循环因果模型中的有效性。

Method: 基于条件独立性信息的局部协变量选择方法；利用σ-无环化（σ-acyclification）操作下条件独立断言的不变性，将循环因果模型归约到无环情形进行分析。

Result: 证明了该方法在循环因果模型中保持完整性和完备性；通过合成数据实验验证了在循环模型中的可靠性能。

Conclusion: 该局部方法适用于循环与无环因果设置，无需修改即可统一使用，为因果效应估计提供了一种对循环结构无偏见的统一视角。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects from observational data requires identifying valid adjustment sets. This task is especially challenging in realistic settings where latent confounding and feedback loops are present. Existing approaches typically assume acyclicity or rely on global causal structure learning, limiting applicability and computational efficiency. In this work, we study a local, data-driven method for covariate selection based on conditional independence information. While this method is known to be sound and complete in acyclic causal models, its validity in the presence of cycles has remained unclear. Our main contribution is to show that these guarantees extend to cyclic causal models. In particular, our result relies on the invariance of conditional independence assertions under $σ$-acyclification. These findings establish a unified, cycle-agnostic perspective on covariate selection and causal effect estimation, showing that the method applies across cyclic and acyclic settings without modification. Empirically, we validate this on extensive synthetic data, showing reliable performance in cyclic causal models.

</details>


### [280] [Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation: Bridging Exploitation and Imitation at the Token Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06387)
*Nan Jia,Haojin Yang,Xing Ma,Jiesong Lian,Shuailiang Zhang,Weipeng Zhang,Ke Zeng,Xunliang Cai,Zequn Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称同策略蒸馏（AOPD），通过将非正优势区域的负强化替换为局部散度最小化，解决了同策略蒸馏中高方差更新、零优势区域梯度消失和探索瓶颈三个结构性问题，在数学推理基准上相比标准OPD平均提升4.09/8.34（强/弱初始化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准同策略蒸馏（OPD）的优势加权策略梯度存在三个结构性缺陷：高方差更新、零优势区域梯度消失、以及纠正信号不足时的探索瓶颈，这些问题限制了蒸馏性能。

Method: 提出了非对称同策略蒸馏（AOPD），核心思想是非对称处理：在正优势区域保留正强化学习，在非正优势区域将无效的负强化替换为局部散度最小化。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，AOPD一致优于标准OPD，强初始化下平均提升4.09，弱初始化下平均提升8.34；同时保持更高的策略熵和更好的工具序贯适应能力保持。

Conclusion: AOPD通过非对称设计有效克服了OPD的结构性缺陷，在性能、探索能力和能力保持方面均取得显著提升。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own trajectories with token-level teacher feedback and often outperforms off-policy distillation and standard reinforcement learning. However, we find that its standard advantage weighted policy gradient suffers from three structural weaknesses, including high variance updates, vanishing gradients in zero-advantage regions, and exploration bottlenecks when corrective signals are insufficient.We therefore propose Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation (AOPD), which replaces ineffective negative reinforcement with localized divergence minimization in non-positive advantage regions while preserving positive reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that AOPD consistently outperforms standard OPD, with average gains of 4.09 / 8.34 under strong / weak initialization, respectively. AOPD also maintains higher policy entropy during training and better capability retention during sequential tool-use adaptation.

</details>


### [281] [SparseForge: Efficient Semi-Structured LLM Sparsification via Annealing of Hessian-Guided Soft-Mask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06402)
*Liu Hanzuo,Chaofan Lin,Weixuan Sun,Yulong Wang,Key,Rayying,Mingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SparseForge是一种后训练半结构化剪枝框架，通过直接优化稀疏掩码（结合Hessian感知重要性估计和渐进退火软掩码）替代大规模重训练，在仅用5B tokens的情况下使LLaMA-2-7B在2:4稀疏度下达到57.27%的零样本准确率，超越稠密模型（56.43%）并接近使用40B tokens的SOTA方法（57.52%），显著提升了精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半结构化剪枝后训练方法因结构耦合导致精度严重下降，需要大规模稀疏重训练（如40B tokens）来恢复精度，计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出SparseForge框架，核心创新包括：(1) Hessian感知的重要性估计用于评估剪枝影响；(2) 渐进退火软掩码机制，将软掩码逐步转化为硬件可执行的半结构化稀疏模式，从而直接优化稀疏掩码而非扩大重训练数据量。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B上2:4稀疏度下，SparseForge仅用5B retraining tokens即达57.27%零样本准确率，超越稠密模型（56.43%），接近SOTA方法（57.52%，需40B tokens），且该优势在多个模型族中保持一致。

Conclusion: 通过直接优化稀疏掩码而非扩大重训练规模，可在极低计算成本下实现接近甚至超越稠密模型的精度，为半结构化剪枝的高效后训练恢复提供了新范式。

Abstract: Semi-structured sparsity provides a practical path to accelerate large language models (LLMs) with native hardware support, but post-training semi-structured pruning often suffers from substantial quality degradation due to strong structural coupling. Existing methods rely on large-scale sparse retraining to recover accuracy, resulting in high computational cost.
  We propose SparseForge, a post-training framework that improves recovery efficiency by directly optimizing the sparsity mask rather than scaling up retraining tokens. SparseForge combines Hessian-aware importance estimation with progressive annealing of soft masks into hardware-executable structured sparsity, enabling stable and efficient sparse recovery. On LLaMA-2-7B under 2:4 sparsity, SparseForge achieves 57.27% average zero-shot accuracy with only $\textbf{5B}$ retraining tokens, surpassing the dense model's 56.43% accuracy and approaching the 57.52% result of a state-of-the-art method using $\textbf{40B}$ tokens. Such improvements on the accuracy-efficiency trade-off from SparseForge are shown to be consistent across model families.

</details>


### [282] [FRInGe: Distribution-Space Integrated Gradients with Fisher--Rao Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06404)
*Gabriele Martino,Sebastian Tschiatschek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FRInGe方法，在预测分布空间而非输入空间定义参考点和插值路径，通过Fisher-Rao测地线和KL/欧几里得信任区域稳定归因，显著提升归因校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有积分梯度(IG)方法因依赖启发式基线、直线路径、离散化和饱和问题而存在脆弱性，需要更鲁棒且模型忠实的归因方法。

Method: 在预测分布空间中定义最大熵参考点，沿Fisher-Rao测地线在概率单纯形上插值，通过拉回Fisher度量实现输入空间轨迹，并用KL散度和欧几里得信任区域稳定该轨迹，最后沿此轨迹积分输入梯度得到归因。

Result: 在六种ImageNet架构上，FRInGe显著提升校准导向的归因指标（尤其MAS分数），同时在扰动AUC和infidelity指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 在预测分布空间进行归因能有效克服传统IG的脆弱性，提高归因的校准性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Gradient-based attribution methods are model-faithful and scalable, but Integrated Gradients (IG) can be brittle because explanations depend on heuristic baselines, straight-line paths, discretization, and saturation. We propose Fisher--Rao Integrated Gradients (FRInGe), which defines both the reference and interpolation schedule in predictive distribution space. FRInGe replaces input baselines with a maximum-entropy predictive reference and follows a Fisher-Rao geodesic on the probability simplex. The corresponding input-space trajectory is realized through the pullback Fisher metric and stabilized by KL and Euclidean trust regions; attributions are obtained by integrating input gradients along this trajectory. Across six ImageNet architectures, FRInGe most clearly improves calibration-oriented attribution metrics, especially MAS scores, while remaining competitive on perturbation AUC and infidelity.

</details>


### [283] [Federated Cross-Client Subgraph Pattern Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06433)
*Selin Ceydeli,Rui Wang,Kubilay Atasu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对分布式图场景下GNN子图模式检测存在的表示等价差距（representation-equivalence gap），本文将其形式化为结构可观测性问题，并提出逐层嵌入交换框架，通过每步（per-step）同步中间节点表示来恢复与集中式GNN等价的表示，实验证明嵌入交换与联邦参数聚合互补而非可互换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN-based子图模式检测方法假设图数据集中存储，但实际图数据常分布在多方。分布式场景下，各客户端的本地GNN计算与集中式模型产生偏差，导致表示等价差距（representation-equivalence gap），即跨分区边界的子图模式在局部不可识别。

Method: 将表示等价差距形式化为结构可观测性问题（structural observability problem）；提出per-step、layer-wise的嵌入交换框架，客户端在前向传播的每一层同步中间节点表示，不暴露原始特征和标签；要求满足扩展子图假设（extended-subgraph assumption）且跨客户端共享模型参数。

Result: 在合成有向多重图（含环、双团、scatter-gather模式）上实验表明：嵌入交换与联邦参数聚合互补而非可互换；两者结合可恢复大部分表示差距，前提是交换的嵌入是每步新鲜（per-step）而非每轮陈旧（per-epoch）。

Conclusion: 所提框架在分布式图设置下能恢复与集中式GNN等价的节点表示，为跨多方协同的子图模式检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Subgraph pattern detection aims to uncover complex interaction structures in graphs. However, state-of-the-art graph neural network (GNN)-based solutions assume centralized access to the entire graph. When graphs are instead distributed across multiple parties, client-local GNN computations diverge from those of a centralized model, resulting in a representation-equivalence gap. We formalize this as a structural observability problem, where subgraph patterns crossing partition boundaries become locally unidentifiable. To bridge this gap, we propose a per-step, layer-wise embedding exchange framework in which clients synchronize intermediate node representations at each layer of the forward pass, without exposing raw features or labels. Under an extended-subgraph assumption and shared model parameters across clients, this framework recovers the same node representations as a centralized GNN over the full graph. Experiments on synthetic directed multigraphs with cycles, bicliques, and scatter-gather patterns show that embedding exchange and federated parameter aggregation are complementary rather than interchangeable: their combination recovers most of the representation gap, provided exchanged embeddings are fresh per-step rather than stale per-epoch.

</details>


### [284] [FedFrozen: Two-Stage Federated Optimization via Attention Kernel Freezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06446)
*Junye Du,Zhenghao Li,Yushi Feng,Long Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedFrozen，一种两阶段联邦优化框架：先全模型热身训练，再冻结注意力机制中的query/key块、仅优化value块。理论分析表明热身阶段可视为正则化核轮廓目标的不精确下降过程，冻结阶段简化为固定注意力核下的受限value块优化，并揭示了热身时长的权衡。实验验证了该方法在异构联邦学习中提升Transformer模型的稳定性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦优化方法主要通过目标级正则化或更新校正机制解决客户端漂移问题，但Transformer架构在异构联邦训练中表现出更强的鲁棒性。受此启发，作者探究注意力机制中不同参数组件对联邦优化的影响。

Method: 将注意力模块分解为query/key块（决定注意力核）和value块（在诱导核下执行语义变换），提出FedFrozen两阶段框架：阶段一进行全模型热身训练；阶段二冻结query/key块，仅优化value块。在线性注意力公式下进行理论分析。

Result: 仿真实验验证了预测的偏置-漂移行为，真实数据实验表明FedFrozen在异构联邦学习中提升了Transformer模型的稳定性和有效性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦注意力机制中不同组件的角色并设计针对性的冻结策略，能够有效缓解异构联邦学习中的客户端漂移问题，且理论分析揭示了热身时长的显式权衡。

Abstract: Federated learning with heterogeneous clients remains a significant challenge for deep learning, primarily due to client drift arising from inconsistent local updates. Existing federated optimization methods typically address this issue through objective-level regularization or update-correction mechanisms. Recent studies, however, suggest that Transformer-based architectures may be inherently more robust than conventional models under heterogeneous federated training. Motivated by this observation, we investigate how different parameter components within the attention mechanism influence federated optimization. Specifically, we decompose the attention module into a query/key block, which determines the attention kernel, and a value block, which performs semantic transformation under the induced kernel. Based on this perspective, we propose FedFrozen, a two-stage federated optimization framework that first performs full-model warm-up training and then freezes the query/key block while continuing to optimize the value block. Under a linear-attention formulation, we show that the warm-up stage can be interpreted as an inexact descent procedure on a regularized kernel-profile objective, while the frozen stage reduces to a restricted value-block optimization problem under a fixed attention kernel. Our analysis further reveals an explicit trade-off that governs the choice of warm-up length. Simulations validate the predicted bias-drift behavior, and real-data experiments demonstrate that FedFrozen improves both the stability and effectiveness of Transformer models in heterogeneous federated learning.

</details>


### [285] [Scene-Adaptive Continual Learning for CSI-based Human Activity Recognition with Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06447)
*Wenhan Zheng,Yuyi Mao,Ivan Wang-Hei Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAMoE-C方法，通过混合专家系统与注意力路由机制解决CSI人体活动识别中的跨域持续学习问题，仅需少量重放缓冲即可实现场景自适应，在降低推理成本的同时保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于CSI的人体活动识别在不同物理环境（域迁移）下性能严重下降，现有持续学习方案存在扩展性差、依赖大量重放缓冲或推理成本线性增长等问题。

Method: 提出Scene-Adaptive Mixture of Experts with Clustered Specialists (SAMoE-C)，将跨域CSI-HAR构建为混合专家系统，通过基于注意力的语义路由器为每个输入激活特定专家，并开发仅需小量重放缓冲的新型训练协议来稳定路由器的域判别能力。

Result: 在四场景CSI数据集上，SAMoE-C达到与最先进方法相当的精度，同时保持显著更低的推理成本。

Conclusion: SAMoE-C通过模块化专家、选择性激活与轻量训练协议的联合设计，实现了可扩展的跨域CSI-HAR部署，兼具低训练开销和高计算效率，适用于实际场景。

Abstract: Channel state information (CSI)-based human activity recognition (HAR) is vulnerable to performance degradation under domain shifts across varying physical environments. Continual learning (CL) offers a principled way to learn new domains sequentially while preserving past knowledge, but existing CL solutions for CSI-based HAR scale poorly with accumulating domains, rely on a large replay buffer, or incur linearly growing inference cost. In this letter, we propose Scene-Adaptive Mixture of Experts with Clustered Specialists (SAMoE-C), which formulates cross-domain CSI-based HAR as a mixture-of-experts system that enables scene-specific adaptation, via an attention-based semantic router that activates only selected experts for each input. Moreover, we develop a novel training protocol, which requires only a tiny replay buffer for stabilizing domain discrimination of the router. Experimental results on a four-scene CSI dataset demonstrate that SAMoE-C approaches the state-of-the-art accuracy, while maintaining a significantly lower inference cost. By jointly combining modular experts, selective activation with router and a lightweight training protocol, SAMoE-C enables scalable cross-domain CSI-based HAR deployment with low training overhead and high computational efficiency in real-world settings.

</details>


### [286] [Diversity Curves for Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06466)
*Katharina Limbeck,Nadja Häusermann,Martin Carrasco,Guy Wolf,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多样性曲线（diversity curves）作为图级别表示方法，通过追踪图在不同粗化层级上的结构多样性生成可解释、可比较的嵌入，并证明边收缩粗化能提升表达能力，在多个任务上超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图级别表示方法在处理不同基数的图比较时尚存挑战，尤其无监督任务需要可解释、可扩展且对规模敏感的图表示，现有结构描述符难以满足这些需求。

Method: 提出多样性曲线（diversity curves）作为图嵌入方法。具体而言：（1）追踪图在粗化层级上的结构多样性；（2）引入spread（离散度）这一新的等距不变量，用于编码图的度量多样性和几何信息；（3）采用边收缩粗化策略，并理论上证明其能提升表达能力。

Result: 在四个任务上验证有效性：（i）对不同规模的模拟图进行聚类和可视化；（ii）区分单细胞图的几何结构；（iii）比较分子图数据集的结构；（iv）表征几何形状。实验表明多样性曲线优于多种基线方法。

Conclusion: 多样性曲线是一种可解释、高效且可直接比较的图级别表示方法，通过粗化层次上的结构追踪和边收缩策略，在多个图分析任务中展现出优于传统结构描述符的性能。

Abstract: Graph-level representations are crucial tools for characterising structural differences between graphs. However, comparing graphs with different cardinalities, even when sampled from the same underlying distribution, remains challenging. Unsupervised tasks in particular require interpretable, scalable, and reliable size-aware graph representations. Our work addresses these issues by tracking the structural diversity of a graph across coarsening levels. The resulting graph embeddings, which we denote diversity curves, are interpretable by construction, efficient, and directly comparable across coarsening hierarchies. Specifically, we track the spread of graphs, a novel isometry invariant that is inherently well-suited for encoding the metric diversity and geometry of graphs. We utilise edge contraction coarsening and prove that this improves expressivity, thus leading to more powerful graph-level representations than structural descriptors alone. Demonstrating their utility over a range of baseline methods in practice, we use diversity curves to (i) cluster and visualise simulated graphs across varying sizes, (ii) distinguish the geometry of single-cell graphs, (iii) compare the structure of molecular graph datasets, and (iv) characterise geometric shapes.

</details>


### [287] [No Triangulation Without Representation: Generalization in Topological Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06467)
*Johannes S. Schmidt,Martin Carrasco,Ernst Röell,Guy Wolf,Nello Blaser,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了MANTRA基准数据集至更多样化的流形类别，发现GNN和HOMP模型在该基准上均能达到饱和，但依赖于正确的表示和特征分配。通过提出基于表示多样性和三角剖分细化的新评估协议，发现现有模型无法泛化到数据组合结构之外，表明需要开发理解拓扑结构的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑深度学习模型缺乏统一的评估标准，且拓扑对象允许图数据中不存在的操作（如结构细化），导致评估困难。

Method: 将MANTRA基准扩展到更多样化的流形同胚类型；提出基于表示多样性和三角剖分细化的新评估协议。

Result: GNN和HOMP方法在正确表示和特征分配下均可饱和该基准；但现有模型无法泛化到数据组合结构之外。

Conclusion: 现有模型缺乏对拓扑结构尺度不变的理解能力，需要开发拓扑感知的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Despite an ever-increasing interest in topological deep learning models that target higher-order datasets, there is no consensus on how to evaluate such models. This is exacerbated by the fact that topological objects permit operations, such as structural refinements, that are not appropriate for graph data. In this work, we extend MANTRA, a benchmark dataset containing manifold triangulations, to a larger class of manifolds with more diverse homeomorphism types. We show that, unlike prior claims, both graph neural networks (GNNs) and higher-order message passing (HOMP) methods can saturate the benchmark. However, we find that this is contingent on the right representation and feature assignment, emphasizing their importance in baseline models. We thus provide a novel evaluation protocol based on representational diversity and triangulation refinement. Surprisingly, we find no indication that existing models are capable of generalizing beyond the combinatorial structure of the data. This points towards a research gap in developing models that understand topological structure independent of scale. Our work thus provides the necessary scaffolding to evaluate future models and enable the development of topology-aware inductive biases.

</details>


### [288] [Hitting Time Isomorphism for Multi-Stage Planning with Foundation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06470)
*Magnus Victor Boock,Abdullah Akgül,Mustafa Mert Çelikok,Melih Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的基于算子理论的无监督表示学习框架，用于离线强化学习。该框架从首次击中时间观测中恢复受控马尔可夫过程的定向时序几何结构，并证明了在潜在线性闭包条件下表示的存在性与唯一可辨识性。基于该理论，作者设计了同构嵌入学习（IEL）算法，通过显式的首次击中时间回归与HILP风格的一致性目标，学习非对称且可组合的潜在几何结构，从而支持基于图的多阶段长程规划。实验表明，IEL在离线迷宫 locomotion 数据上达到了最先进的基础策略学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法往往产生对称距离或无法满足三角不等式，无法正确捕捉受控马尔可夫过程的定向时序几何。本文旨在提出一个能够在Hilbert空间中恢复有向时间序几何的表示学习框架，使期望击中时间能表示为潜在位移的线性泛函，从而支持长程导航中的多阶段规划。

Method: 1) 提出基于算子理论的表示学习框架，将期望击中时间实现为Hilbert空间中潜在位移的线性泛函；2) 证明在潜在线性闭包条件下表示存在且唯一（至多相差一个有界线性同构）；3) 给出有限维实现中全局击中时间误差被一步转移误差与环境暂态谱半径乘积所界的理论保证；4) 提供包含逼近误差、统计复杂度和轨迹标签不匹配的有限样本保证；5) 设计同构嵌入学习（IEL）算法，结合HILP风格一致性目标与显式击中时间回归。

Result: 理论结果表明：(a) 表示存在且可唯一辨识；(b) 全局误差由一步误差与谱半径乘积界定；(c) 给出有限样本保证。实验结果表明IEL在离线迷宫locomotion数据上达到了最先进的基础策略学习性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的IEL算法通过学习非对称且可组合的潜在几何结构，有效实现了基于图的多阶段长程规划，在离线强化学习的基础策略学习任务上超越了现有方法。

Abstract: We present a new operator-theoretic representation learning framework for offline reinforcement learning that recovers the directed temporal geometry of a controlled Markov process from hitting time observations. While prior art often produces symmetric distances or fails to satisfy the triangle inequality, our framework learns a Hilbert-space displacement geometry where expected hitting times are realized as linear functionals of latent displacements. We prove that this representation exists under latent linear closure and is uniquely identifiable up to a bounded linear isomorphism. For finite-dimensional implementations, we show that global hitting-time error is bounded by one-step transition error amplified by the environment's transient spectral radius. Furthermore, we provide finite-sample guarantees accounting for approximation, statistical complexity, and trajectory-label mismatch. Derived from this theory, we curate Isomorphic Embedding Learning (IEL) as a new goal-agnostic foundation policy learning algorithm that anchors a HILP-style consistency objective with explicit hitting-time regression to ensure that the learned geometry reflects actual decision-time progress. This asymmetric and compositional structure enables robust graph-based multi-stage planning for long-horizon navigation. Our experiments demonstrate that IEL improves the state of the art of learning foundation policy policies from offline maze locomotion data. Our code can be found on https://github.com/MagnusBoock/IEL

</details>


### [289] [Efficient Serving for Dynamic Agent Workflows with Prediction-based KV-Cache Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06472)
*Haoyu Zheng,Fangcheng Fu,Jia Wu,Binhang Yuan,Yongqiang Zhang,Hao Wang,Yuanyuan Zhu,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PBKV是一种基于预测的KV缓存管理系统，通过预测动态工作流中未来步骤的智能体调用来优化缓存复用，在动态工作流上相比LRU实现最高1.85倍加速，在静态工作流上相比SOTA基线KVFlow实现最高1.26倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么在智能体级别管理KV缓存，无法利用工作流内的复用机会；要么在工作流级别管理，但假设工作流调用静态的智能体序列。实际工作流通常是动态的，智能体调用序列和缓存复用机会取决于任务上下文，因此需要一种能适应动态工作流的KV缓存管理方法。

Method: PBKV通过融合历史工作流的引导和目标工作流的上下文，预测未来若干步骤中的智能体调用；基于预测评估缓存条目的复用潜力，将高潜力条目保留在GPU内存中；在缓存驱逐和预取时保守地利用预测结果以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在三个工作流基准测试上，PBKV在动态工作流上相比LRU实现最高1.85倍加速，在静态工作流上相比SOTA基线KVFlow实现最高1.26倍加速。

Conclusion: 基于预测的KV缓存管理能有效服务于动态工作流，通过预测与保守策略的结合，在提升缓存命中率的同时保持对预测误差的鲁棒性。

Abstract: LLM-based workflows compose specialized agents to execute complex tasks, and these agents usually share substantial context, allowing KV-Cache reuse to save computation. Existing approaches either manage KV-Cache at agent level and fail to exploit the reuse opportunities within workflows, or manage cache at the workflow level but assume that each workflow calls a static sequence of agents. However, practical workflows are typically dynamic, where the sequence of invoked agents and thus induced cache reuse opportunities depend on the context of each task. To serve such dynamic workflows efficiently, we build a system dubbed PBKV (\textbf{P}rediction-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{KV}-Cache Management). For each workflow, PBKV predicts the agent invocations in several future steps by fusing the guidance from historical workflows and context of the target workflow. Based on the predictions, PBKV estimates the reuse potential of cache entries and keeps the high-potential entries in GPU memory. To be robust to prediction errors, PBKV utilizes the predictions conservatively during both cache eviction and prefetching. Experiments on three workflow benchmarks show that PBKV achieves up to $1.85\times$ speedup over LRU on dynamic workflows, and up to $1.26\times$ speedup over the SOTA baseline KVFlow on the static workflow.

</details>


### [290] [Q-MMR: Off-Policy Evaluation via Recursive Reweighting and Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06474)
*Xiang Li,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-MMR框架，通过矩匹配学习数据点权重实现离策略评估，在仅需Q^π可实现性条件下获得无维度依赖的有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有限 horizon MDP 中离策略评估问题，现有方法在函数逼近下要么依赖强覆盖假设，要么误差受函数类统计复杂度影响，缺乏更优的保证。

Method: Q-MMR框架：为每个数据点学习标量权重，通过自上而下的归纳式矩匹配目标，以价值函数判别器类为对抗，使得重加权奖励逼近目标策略下的期望回报。

Result: 在仅需Q^π可实现性条件下，建立了数据依赖的有限样本保证，且误差界无维度依赖（不依赖于函数类的统计复杂度）；与重要性采样、线性FQE等方法建立了联系；对覆盖度概念提供了新见解。

Conclusion: Q-MMR为离策略评估提供了新的理论框架，在弱假设下实现了强保证，深化了对离线RL中覆盖度本质的理解。

Abstract: We present a novel theoretical framework, Q-MMR, for off-policy evaluation in finite-horizon MDPs. Q-MMR learns a set of scalar weights, one for each data point, such that the reweighted rewards approximate the expected return under the target policy. The weights are learned inductively in a top-down manner via a moment matching objective against a value-function discriminator class. Notably, and perhaps surprisingly, a data-dependent finite-sample guarantee for general function approximation can be established under only the realizability of $Q^π$, with a dimension-free bound -- that is, the error does not depend on the statistical complexity of the function class. We also establish connections to several existing methods, such as importance sampling and linear FQE. Further theoretical analyses shed new light on the nature of coverage, a concept of fundamental importance to offline RL.

</details>


### [291] [Operator-Guided Invariance Learning for Continuous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06500)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Fei Xu Yu,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VPSD-RL框架，通过Lie群作用和拉回算子在连续时间/状态/动作空间的强化学习中自动发现精确或近似的保价值结构，从而提高数据效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间/状态/动作空间的强化学习往往数据密集且对 nuisance 变异和偏移脆弱，现有方法多局限于预设对称性和精确等变性，未能解决如何发现需要非线性算子来变换和映射具有同构价值函数的连续系统的更一般结构问题。

Method: 将连续RL建模为受控扩散过程，通过Lie群作用和拉回算子定义保价值映射；证明保价值结构存在当且仅当拉回价值函数与前推动作与控制生成器和奖励泛函可交换；通过Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman残差最小化找到近似保价值结构；拟合可微漂移、扩散和奖励模型，通过确定方程残差最小化学习无穷小生成元，用ODE流指数化得到有限变换，通过变换增强和变换一致性正则化集成到连续RL中。

Result: 有界生成器/奖励失配意味着最优价值函数沿近似轨道的定量稳定性，敏感性由有效视界控制；在连续控制基准上观察到数据效率和鲁棒性提升。
