Conclusion: VPSD-RL能够自动发现连续RL中的精确和近似保价值结构，有效提升数据效率和鲁棒性，为结构发现提供了理论保证和实用框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with continuous time and state/action spaces is often data-intensive and brittle under nuisance variability and shift, motivating methods that exploit value-preserving structures to stabilize and improve learning. Most existing approaches focus on special cases, such as prescribed symmetries and exact equivariance, without addressing how to discover more general structures that require nonlinear operators to transform and map between continuous state/action systems with isomorphic value functions. We propose \textbf{VPSD-RL} (Value-Preserving Structure Discovery for Reinforcement Learning). It models continuous RL as a controlled diffusion with value-preserving mappings defined through Lie-group actions and associated pullback operators. We show that a value-preserving structure exists exactly when pulling back the value function and pushing forward actions commute with the controlled generator and reward functional. Further, approximate value-preserving structures with rigorous guarantees can be found when the Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman mismatch is small. This framework discovers exact and approximate value-preserving structures by searching for the associated Lie group operators. VPSD-RL fits differentiable drift, diffusion, and reward models; learns infinitesimal generators via determining-equation residual minimization; exponentiates them with ODE flows to obtain finite transformations; and integrates them into continuous RL through transition augmentation and transformation-consistency regularization. We show that bounded generator/reward mismatch implies quantitative stability of the optimal value function along approximate orbits, with sensitivity governed by the effective horizon, and observe improved data efficiency and robustness on continuous-control benchmarks.

</details>


### [292] [PACZero: PAC-Private Fine-Tuning of Language Models via Sign Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06505)
*Murat Bilgehan Ertan,Xiaochen Zhu,Phuong Ha Nguyen,Marten van Dijk,Srinivas Devadas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACZero提出了一系列PAC私有零阶机制，用于大语言模型微调，能在互信息$I=0$（对应DP中$\varepsilon=0$的隐私级别）下实现可用效用。核心思想是利用符号量化子集聚合的零阶梯度产生一致性步（所有候选子集更新方向一致），此时释放的符号零条件互信息。在SST-2和SQuAD上使用OPT-1.3B和OPT-6.7B评估，$I=0$时SST-2准确率可达88.99%，距非私有MeZO基线仅差2.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP框架在$\varepsilon<1$的高隐私区间无法产生可用效用（需无限噪声），而PAC Privacy允许在成员推理攻击（MIA）后验成功率等于先验的严格隐私水平下（即DP框架仅在$\varepsilon=0$时才能达到的水平）实现有用微调。目标是设计一种在该隐私级别下仍能保持实用性的微调方法。

Method: 基于PAC Privacy框架，对零阶梯度进行子集聚合后符号量化。当所有候选子集对更新方向一致时（一致性步），释放的符号为零条件互信息；不一致时通过均匀抛硬币决定（PACZero-ZPL）或精确校准二元释放的互信息预算（PACZero-MI）。

Result: 在SST-2上OPT-1.3B全参数微调且$I=0$时，PACZero-ZPL达到88.99±0.91%准确率，仅比非私有MeZO基线（91.1%）低2.1个百分点。在SQuAD上也取得非平凡F1分数。此前没有方法能在$\varepsilon<1$的高隐私区间产生可用效用。

Conclusion: PACZero证明了在严格的PAC隐私框架下（互信息$I=0$，对应DP $\varepsilon=0$），通过利用零阶梯度聚合中的一致性现象，可以实现以前认为不可能的有用微调性能，为高隐私需求的LLM微调提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce PACZero, a family of PAC-private zeroth-order mechanisms for fine-tuning large language models that delivers usable utility at $I(S^*; Y_{1:T})=0$. This privacy regime bounds the membership-inference attack (MIA) posterior success rate at the prior, an MIA-resistance level the DP framework matches only at $\varepsilon=0$ and infinite noise. All DP-ZO comparisons below are matched at the MIA posterior level. The key insight is that PAC Privacy charges mutual information only when the release depends on which candidate subset is the secret. Sign-quantizing subset-aggregated zeroth-order gradients creates frequent unanimity, steps at which every candidate subset agrees on the update direction; at these steps the released sign costs zero conditional mutual information. We propose two variants that span the privacy-utility trade-off: PACZero-MI (budgeted MI via exact calibration on the binary release) and PACZero-ZPL ($I=0$ via a uniform coin flip on disagreement steps). We evaluate on SST-2 and SQuAD with OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B in both LoRA and full-parameter tracks. On SST-2 OPT-1.3B full fine-tuning at $I=0$, PACZero-ZPL reaches ${88.99\pm0.91}$, within $2.1$pp of the non-private MeZO baseline ($91.1$ FT). No prior method produces usable utility in the high-privacy regime $\varepsilon<1$, and PACZero-ZPL obtains competitive SST-2 accuracy and nontrivial SQuAD F1 across OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B at $I=0$.

</details>


### [293] [Is One Layer Enough? Understanding Inference Dynamics in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06510)
*Amir Rezaei Balef,Mykhailo Koshil,Katharina Eggensperger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模研究6种表格上下文学习模型的逐层机制，发现深度冗余和重叠计算，并据此设计了一个仅用20%参数的循环单层模型，性能与原始模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFMs）在小到中型表格预测任务中表现优异，但其推理机制尚未被深入理解，缺乏对其逐层动态和内部工作原理的探索。

Method: 对6种最先进的表格上下文学习模型进行大规模机械论研究，分析层间动态、预测涌现过程、推理阶段划分及潜在空间动力学，并基于发现的深度冗余设计循环单层模型。

Result: 发现模型存在显著的深度冗余，推理呈现出迭代精炼和重叠计算的模式；基于此设计的循环单层模型仅需原始模型20%的参数，即可达到可比性能。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型的推理机制与语言模型不同，深度存在冗余，可通过循环单层架构大幅压缩参数而不显著损失性能。

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models (TFMs) dominate small to medium tabular predictive benchmark tasks, yet their inference mechanisms remain largely unexplored. We present the first large-scale mechanistic study of layerwise dynamics in 6 state-of-the-art tabular in-context learning models. We explore how predictions emerge across depth, identify distinct stages of inference and reveal latent-space dynamics that differ from those of language models. Our findings indicate substantial depthwise redundancy across multiple models, suggesting iterative refinement with overlapping computations during inference stages. Guided by these insights, we design a proof-of-concept, looped single-layer model that uses only 20% of the original model's parameters while achieving comparable performance. The code is available at https://github.com/amirbalef/is_one_layer_enough.

</details>


### [294] [On the Implicit Reward Overfitting and the Low-rank Dynamics in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06523)
*Hao Ye,Jisheng Dang,Junfeng Fang,Bimei Wang,Yizhou Zhang,Ning Lv,Wencan Zhang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现RLVR训练中模型主要通过秩1分量获取推理能力，并揭示了隐含奖励过拟合、奇异谱重尾分布、左奇异向量对齐增强等反直觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明RLVR增强的推理能力主要集中在秩1分量上，但对其具体机制和潜在问题缺乏深入理解。本文旨在揭示RLVR如何塑造模型参数及其反直觉特性。

Method: 采用周期性秩1替换（Periodic Rank-1 Substitution）方法，观察RLVR训练过程中秩1分量的演变，分析奇异值分布、对齐趋势等谱特性。

Result: 1) RLVR存在隐含奖励过拟合：训练集奖励较低时测试集性能已满意；2) 秩1分量仅保留数学推理能力，不维持其他知识；3) RLVR优化特定奇异谱，几乎所有线性层奇异值呈重尾分布；4) 左奇异向量在训练中对齐增强，RLVR本质优化采样效率。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了RLVR的参数塑造机制，为改进现有RL范式或实现持续学习提供了潜在见解。

Abstract: Recent extensive research has demonstrated that the enhanced reasoning capabilities acquired by models through Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) are primarily concentrated within the rank-1 components. Predicated on this observation, we employed Periodic Rank-1 Substitution and identified a counterintuitive phenomenon: RLVR may exhibit implicit reward overfitting to the training dataset. Specifically, the model can achieve satisfactory performance on the test set even when its rewards remain relatively low during the training process. Furthermore, we characterize three distinct properties of RL training: (1) The effective rank-1 component in RLVR don't maintain other model knowledge except mathematical reasoning capability. (2) RLVR fundamentally functions by optimizing a specific singular spectrum. The distribution of singular values of almost all linear layers in RLVR-trained model behaves like heavy-tailed distribution. (3) the left singular vectors associated with rank-1 components demonstrate a stronger alignment tendency during training, which echoes the discovery that RLVR is optimizing sampling efficiency in essence. Taken together, our findings and analysis further reveal how RLVR shapes model parameters and offer potential insights for improving existing RL paradigms or other training paradigms to implement continual learning.

</details>


### [295] [Diffusion-Based Posterior Sampling: A Feynman-Kac Analysis of Bias and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06538)
*Matias G. Delgadino,Sebastien Motsch,Advait Parulekar,William Porteous,Sanjay Shakkottai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入可处理的替代路径和Feynman-Kac表示，系统地表征了基于扩散的后验采样器（如DPS和STSL）的偏差，并解释了现有纠正方法及低温不稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散后验采样器广泛用于逆问题，但其理论行为（偏差来源、低温不稳定性和离散化误差）尚不明确，亟需严格的理论分析框架。

Method: 通过构造真后验到标准高斯的可替代路径与采样器路径进行对比，推导密度比满足的抛物型PDE（反应项度量累积偏差），并利用Feynman-Kac表示将Radon-Nikodym修正表达为显式路径期望。将该框架应用于DPS和STSL，分析其偏差特性；并分析早期引导停止对低温不稳定性的缓解机制。

Result: DPS的修正为Ornstein-Uhlenbeck路径期望，耦合数据条件协方差与奖励曲率，揭示过采样/欠采样区域；STSL通过辅助漂移引导轨迹向低不确定性区域，平坦化DPS反应项的空间变化部分；早期引导停止可缓解前向欧拉积分导致的低温不稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架阐明了采样器偏差的理论根源，解释了现有纠正方法的有效性，并为设计稳定变体提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Diffusion-based posterior samplers use pretrained diffusion priors to sample from measurement- or reward-conditioned posteriors, and are widely used for inverse problems. Yet their theoretical behavior remains poorly understood: even with exact prior scores, their outputs are biased, and in low-temperature regimes their discretizations can become unstable. We characterize this bias by introducing a tractable surrogate path connecting the true posterior to a standard Gaussian and comparing it to the sampler's path. Their density ratio satisfies a parabolic PDE whose reaction term measures the accumulated bias. A Feynman-Kac representation then expresses the Radon-Nikodym correction as an explicit path expectation, identifying which posterior regions are over- or under-sampled.
  We apply this framework to DPS and STSL, a related sampler. For DPS, the correction is an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck path expectation coupling the data conditional covariance with the reward curvature, revealing where DPS over- or under-samples. Next, we reinterpret STSL as an auxiliary drift that steers trajectories toward low-uncertainty regions, flattening the spatially varying part of the DPS reaction term. Finally, we characterize early guidance-stopping, a common mitigation for low-temperature instabilities caused by forward-Euler integration of the vector field. Together, these results clarify sampler bias, explain existing correctives, and guide stable variant designs.

</details>


### [296] [Hedging Memory Horizons for Non-Stationary Prediction via Online Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06541)
*Yutong Wang,Yannig Goude,Qiwei Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MELO是一种模型无关的在线聚合方法，通过记忆尺度对冲（对多个遗忘因子下的EWLS专家进行加权聚合），在分布漂移下实现稳定与自适应的平衡，无需先验知道合适的历史记忆长度；在法国电力负荷预测（含COVID-19封锁期）中，相比基线方法RMSE降低34.7%，优于含外部协变量的TabICL模型，且仅需轻量级递归更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线预测面临分布漂移时，预测器需要在平稳期保持稳定、在漂移期快速适应，但最优的历史记忆长度（适应尺度）未知且随时间变化，现有方法通常依赖固定的遗忘因子或需要预先指定记忆长度。

Method: MELO包含两层结构：底层是多个不同遗忘因子的EWLS适应专家（对任何非预见性基预测器池进行指数加权最小二乘自适应），上层使用参数自由的在线聚合规则MLpol对原始预测和EWLS自适应预测进行聚合。该方法无需重新训练模型，仅需每步递归更新。

Result: 在法国全国电力负荷预测（含COVID-19封锁期）实验中，无需任何制度指标、封锁日期或政策协变量，MELO相比仅使用基预测器的MLpol总体RMSE降低34.7%，且总体RMSE低于使用了外部COVID政策响应协变量的TabICL参考模型。

Conclusion: MELO为分布漂移下的在线预测提供了一种实用且理论保证的解决方案：在平静期保持稳定性，在漂移期自动适应，且仅需轻量级计算；确定性oracle不等式表明其能够与最优基预测器及最优有界时变仿射组合竞争，代价仅为路径长度相关的跟踪误差和次线性聚合开销。

Abstract: We study online prediction under distribution shift, where inputs arrive chronologically and outcomes are revealed only after prediction. In this setting, predictors must remain stable in quiet regimes yet adapt when regimes shift, and the right adaptation memory is unknown in advance. We propose MELO (Memory-hedged Exponentially Weighted Least-Squares Online aggregation), a model-agnostic method that hedges across adaptation scales: it wraps any non-anticipating base-predictor pool with exponentially weighted least-squares (EWLS) adaptation experts at multiple forgetting factors, and aggregates raw and EWLS-adapted forecasts with MLpol, a parameter-free online aggregation rule. Under boundedness conditions, we establish deterministic oracle inequalities showing that it competes with both the best raw predictor and the best bounded, time-varying affine combinations of the base predictions, up to a path-length-dependent tracking cost and a sublinear aggregation overhead. We evaluate MELO on French national electricity-load forecasting through the COVID-19 lockdown using no regime indicators, lockdown dates, or policy covariates. MELO reduces overall RMSE by 34.7\% relative to base-only MLpol and achieves lower overall RMSE than a TabICL reference supplied with an external COVID policy-response covariate. Moreover, MELO requires only lightweight per-step recursive updates without model retraining.

</details>


### [297] [Sequential Design of Genetic Circuits Under Uncertainty With Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06552)
*Michal Kobiela,Diego A. Oyarzún,Michael U. Gutmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于强化学习的顺序框架，用于在分子随机性和实验条件变异性双重不确定性下优化基因电路设计。该方法采用预训练的摊销策略，避免传统贝叶斯方法中每轮实验后昂贵的推理和优化步骤，并在异源基因表达和repressilator电路模型中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统设计受困于分子反应的内在随机性和跨实验室/实验条件的变异性带来的不确定性，现有贝叶斯方法虽能处理不确定性但需要每轮实验后进行昂贵的推理和优化，导致实验周期延迟。

Method: 采用基于微分方程或马尔可夫跳跃过程的仿真器模型，结合强化学习策略方法，通过预训练的摊销策略（amortized approach）在参数不确定分布上预先训练，从而在实验周期中无需显式参数推理即可实现基于观测的即时适应。

Result: 在异源基因表达模型和repressilator电路模型上验证，该框架能够有效处理分子噪声和实验室间变异性的双重不确定性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过预训练摊销策略克服了传统贝叶斯方法的计算瓶颈，实现了无需参数推理的即时优化适应，为不确定环境下基因电路设计提供了高效方案。

Abstract: The design of biological systems is hindered by uncertainty arising from both intrinsic stochasticity of biomolecular reactions and variability across laboratory or experimental conditions. In this work, we present a sequential framework to optimize genetic circuits under both forms of uncertainty. By employing simulator models based on differential equations or Markov jump processes alongside a reinforcement learning (RL) policy-based approach, our method suggests experiments that adapt to unknown laboratory conditions while accounting for inherent stochasticity. While previous Bayesian methods address uncertainty through iterative experiment-inference-optimization cycles, they typically require computationally expensive inference and optimization steps after each experimental round, leading to delays. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose an amortized approach trained up-front across a distribution of possible uncertain parameters. This strategy sidesteps the need for explicit parameter inference during the design cycle, enabling immediate, observation-based adaptation. We demonstrate our framework on models for heterologous gene expression and a repressilator circuit, showing that it efficiently handles both molecular noise and cross-laboratory variability.

</details>


### [298] [Diverse Sampling in Diffusion Models with Marginal Preserving Particle Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06553)
*Gal Vinograd,Idan Achituve,Ethan Fetaya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EDDY是一种无需额外训练的引导机制，通过散度自由动力学保持边缘分布不变，同时提升扩散和流匹配模型生成样本的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散和流匹配模型生成的样本多样性不足，需要在保持生成质量的前提下提升样本多样性。

Method: 利用Fokker-Planck方程对称性，通过核函数构建反对称成对矩阵场产生散度自由的无散度扰动，改变粒子轨迹同时保持边缘分布不变；针对文本到图像生成等计算开销大的场景提出实用近似方案。

Result: 在合成分布和文本到图像生成任务上，EDDY在保持强分布保真度的同时显著提升了样本多样性。

Conclusion: EDDY通过散度自由动力学在无需额外训练的条件下有效提升了生成模型的多样性，兼顾了分布保真度和计算效率。

Abstract: We present EDDY (Exact-marginal Diversification via Divergence-free dYnamics), a guidance mechanism for diffusion and flow matching models that promotes diversity among samples generated while maintaining quality. EDDY exploits symmetries of the Fokker-Planck equation, using drift perturbations that change particle trajectories while preserving the evolving marginal distribution. We instantiate this principle through kernel-based anti-symmetric pairwise matrix fields, constructed from the repulsive directions. The resulting divergence-free dynamics promote diversity at the joint particle level while preserving each particle's marginal distribution without any additional training. As computing the guidance can be computationally expensive in cases such as text-to-image generation with perceptual embeddings, we propose practical approximations as an effective and efficient solution. Experiments on synthetic distributions and text-to-image generation show that EDDY improves diversity while maintaining strong distributional fidelity compared to common baselines.

</details>


### [299] [Optimal Counterfactual Search in Tree Ensembles: A Study Across Modeling and Solution Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06561)
*Awa Khouna,Youssouf Emine,Julien Ferry,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对树集成模型的最优反事实解释问题，提出了CPCF（约束规划）方法，通过将数值特征编码为阈值诱导的区间域，离散特征保留有限域表示，构建紧凑的有限域公式。在十个数据集上与MaxSAT和MILP方法对比，结果表明CP整体最优，MaxSAT擅长硬投票集成，MILP在中等分裂层数的摊销推断中具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释的可信度取决于其推荐更改是否真正最小化，次优解释可能导致不必要的昂贵更改，且启发式错误会不均等地影响不同用户。因此需要精确计算最优反事实解释，以保障用户获得公平且可及的补救方案。

Method: 提出CPCF方法，基于约束规划（CP）构建反事实搜索的紧凑有限域公式：数值特征由分裂阈值诱导的区间域编码，离散特征保留原生有限域表示。同时扩展MaxSAT从硬投票到软投票集成，并与MILP最优方法进行系统比较。

Result: 在十个数据集和三类树集成上，CP整体性能最佳；MaxSAT在硬投票集成场景表现最优；MILP在中等分裂层数的摊销推理设置中仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 不同数学规划范式各有适用场景：CP通用性最强，MaxSAT专精于硬投票集成，MILP适合特定条件下的高效推理。研究为选择反事实解释的最优计算框架提供了系统指导。

Abstract: Trust in counterfactual explanations depends critically on whether their recommended changes are truly minimal: suboptimal explanations may vastly overshoot the actual changes needed to alter a decision, and heuristic errors can affect individuals unevenly, giving some users relevant recourse while assigning others unnecessarily costly recommendations. Consequently, we study the problem of computing optimal counterfactual explanations for tree ensembles under plausibility and actionability constraints. This is a combinatorial problem: for a fixed model, counterfactual search boils down to selecting consistent branching decisions and threshold-defined regions under a distance objective. We exploit this structure through CPCF, a constraint programming (CP) formulation in which numerical features are encoded as interval domains induced by split thresholds, while discrete features retain native finite-domain representations. This yields a compact finite-domain formulation that supports multiple distance objectives without continuous split-boundary search. We then place CPCF in a broader comparison across mathematical programming paradigms: we extend a maximum Boolean satisfiability (MaxSAT) formulation, originally designed for hard-voting random forests, to soft-voting ensembles, and compare against the current state-of-the-art mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) optimal approach. Across ten datasets and three types of tree ensembles, we analyze scalability, anytime performance, and sensitivity to distance metrics. We observe that CP achieves the best overall performance. More importantly, our results identify regimes in which the specific strengths of each paradigm make it best suited: CP is most versatile overall, MaxSAT handles hard-voting ensembles particularly well, and MILP remains competitive in amortized inference settings with a moderate number of split levels.

</details>


### [300] [Feature Dimensionality Outweighs Model Complexity in Breast Cancer Subtype Classification Using TCGA-BRCA Gene Expression Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06562)
*Meena Al Hasani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究使用TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，评估了逻辑回归、随机森林和支持向量机在不同特征数量下对乳腺癌亚型分类的性能，发现逻辑回归在亚型层面表现最稳定均衡，而随机森林在少数类亚型上表现不佳，SVM对特征维度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因表达数据具有高维小样本特性，给机器学习模型带来挑战；准确分类乳腺癌亚型对诊断和治疗选择至关重要，需要评估模型复杂度与特征选择对分类性能的影响。

Method: 使用TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，训练逻辑回归、随机森林和支持向量机三种模型；特征数量从50到20,518个高变基因不等；采用分层5折交叉验证，以准确率和宏F1分数评估性能。

Result: 所有模型均取得高准确率，但宏F1分析揭示了亚型层面的显著差异；逻辑回归在亚型间表现最稳定均衡，能改善稀有类别的检测；随机森林尽管总体准确率高，但在少数类亚型上表现不佳；SVM对特征维度敏感。

Conclusion: 在高维生物分类任务中，模型简单性、评估指标选择和特征选择至关重要。

Abstract: Accurate classification of breast cancer subtypes from gene expression data is critical for diagnosis and treatment selection. However, such datasets are characterized by high dimensionality and limited sample size, posing challenges for machine learning models.
  In this study, we evaluate the impact of model complexity and feature selection on subtype classification performance using TCGA-BRCA gene expression data. Logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machine (SVM) models were trained using varying numbers of highly variable genes (50 to 20,518). Performance was evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation and assessed with accuracy and macro F1 score. While all models achieved high accuracy, macro F1 analysis revealed substantial differences in subtype-level performance. Logistic regression demonstrated the most stable and balanced performance across subtypes, including improved detection of rare classes. Random forest underperformed on minority subtypes despite strong overall accuracy, while SVM showed sensitivity to feature dimensionality. These findings highlight the importance of model simplicity, evaluation metrics, and feature selection in high-dimensional biological classification tasks.

</details>


### [301] [SNAPO: Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization for Optimal Control via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06570)
*Dmitri Goloubentsev,Natalija Karpichina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SNAPO是一种将神经策略嵌入可微模拟器的框架，通过平滑近似硬约束和单次伴随梯度传播，实现策略训练与任意数量灵敏度的高效计算，在天然气存储、养老金管理和制药制造三个领域验证了其速度和可扩展性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态规划面临维度灾难，黑盒强化学习训练缓慢且无法提供灵敏度信息，亟需一种既能处理高维状态又能高效计算梯度和灵敏度的方法。

Method: 将神经策略嵌入已知的可微模拟器，用平滑近似替换硬约束，通过单次伴随反向传播计算目标函数对所有策略参数和所有输入的精确梯度。

Result: 天然气存储：训练不到一分钟，可免费获得365条远期曲线灵敏度；养老金管理：灵敏度计算速度比bump-and-revalue快6.5-200倍，且随风险因子数量扩展；制药制造：4单元过程链的跨单元灵敏度及20个ICH Q8监管灵敏度仅需5次伴随传播，耗时74.5毫秒。

Conclusion: 所有灵敏度由训练策略的同一反向传播产生，计算成本仅相当于一次反向传播，与灵敏度数量无关，显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: Many real-world problems require sequential decisions under uncertainty: when to inject or withdraw gas from storage, how to rebalance a pension portfolio each month, what temperature profile to run through a pharmaceutical reactor chain. Dynamic programming solves small instances exactly but scales exponentially in state dimensions. Black-box reinforcement learning handles high-dimensional states but trains slowly and produces no sensitivities. We introduce SNAPO (Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization), a framework that embeds a neural policy inside a known, differentiable simulator, replaces hard constraints with smooth approximations, and computes exact gradients of the objective with respect to all policy parameters and all inputs in a single adjoint pass. We demonstrate SNAPO on three domains: natural gas storage (training in under a minute, 365 forward curve sensitivities at no additional cost per sensitivity), pension fund asset-liability management (6.5x-200x sensitivity speedup over bump-and-revalue, scaling with the number of risk factors), and pharmaceutical manufacturing (cross-unit sensitivities through a 4-unit process chain, with 20 ICH Q8 regulatory sensitivities from 5 adjoint passes in 74.5 milliseconds). All sensitivities are produced by the same backward pass that trains the policy, at a cost proportional to one reverse pass regardless of how many sensitivities are computed.

</details>


### [302] [CLAD: A Clustered Label-Agnostic Federated Learning Framework for Joint Anomaly Detection and Attack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06571)
*Iason Ofeidis,Nikos Papadis,Randeep Bhatia,Leandros Tassiulas,TV Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CLAD框架，融合聚类联邦学习（CFL）与双模式微架构（DM²A），同时解决IoT/IIoT入侵检测中设备异构性和标签稀缺两大瓶颈，在80%客户端无标签场景下实现检测性能相对提升30%、通信成本减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT/IIoT环境存在大规模异构攻击面，传统集中式入侵检测面临隐私风险，标准联邦学习难以泛化到多样设备行为且无法利用海量无标签数据。

Method: 提出CLAD框架：1）双模式微架构（DM²A）包含共享编码器与双分支，同时支持无监督异常检测和有监督攻击分类；2）聚类联邦学习（CFL）动态将流量模式相似的设备分组，防止全局模型发散。

Result: 在80%客户端无标签场景下，检测性能相对提升30%，通信成本仅为基线的一半。

Conclusion: CLAD通过有机结合CFL与DM²A，充分利用了所有数据（无标签和有标签），保留了不同设备的操作模式，显著优于现有最优方法。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and Industrial IoT (IIoT) has created a massive, heterogeneous attack surface that challenges traditional network security mechanisms. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative to centralized Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS), standard approaches struggle to generalize across diverse device behaviors and typically fail to utilize the vast amounts of unlabeled data present in realistic edge environments. To bridge these gaps, we propose CLAD, a holistic framework that seamlessly incorporates Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) with a novel Dual-Mode Micro-Architecture ($\text{DM}^2\text{A}$). This unified approach simultaneously tackles the two primary bottlenecks of IoT security: device heterogeneity and label scarcity. The $\text{DM}^2\text{A}$ component features a shared encoder followed by two branches, enabling joint unsupervised anomaly detection and supervised attack classification; this allows the framework to harvest intelligence from both labeled and unlabeled clients. Concurrently, the clustering component dynamically groups devices with congruent traffic patterns, preventing global model divergence. By carefully combining these elements, CLAD ensures that no data is discarded and distinct operational patterns are preserved. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that this integrated approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 30% relative improvement in detection performance in scenarios with 80% unlabeled clients, with only half the communication cost.

</details>


### [303] [Directional Consistency as a Complementary Optimization Signal: The GONO Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06575)
*Victor Daniel Gera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现深度优化中梯度方向一致性（cc_t）与损失收敛可解耦，现有优化器缺乏利用方向一致性的机制，据此提出GONO优化器，根据cc_t自适应调整Adam的动量系数β₁，理论证明收敛率不变，实验验证方向一致性比梯度范数更有效检测振荡（F1=1.00 vs 0.45），并在多个基准上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器（Adam、SGD、RMSprop）仅依赖梯度幅值信号，无法区分平台区、鞍点和真正收敛，缺乏利用梯度方向时间一致性的显式机制，导致方向对齐与损失收敛可能解耦。

Method: 提出GONO（Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer），基于连续梯度余弦相似度cc_t动态调整Adam的动量系数β₁：方向一致时放大动量，振荡时抑制动量；理论证明其保持O(1/√T)收敛率且在无信息时退化为标准Adam。

Result: cc_t在振荡检测上F1=1.00（梯度范数仅0.45）；GONO在MNIST（98.15%）、CIFAR-10（43.14%）、ResNet-18（75.44%）上与AdamW保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 方向对齐是一种理论上可证、实践上可用的优化信号，GONO通过利用方向一致性有效改进了优化过程。

Abstract: We identify and formalize an underexplored phenomenon in deep learning optimization: directional alignment and loss convergence can be decoupled. An optimizer can exhibit near-perfect directional consistency (cc_t -> 1, measured via consecutive gradient cosine similarity) while the loss remains high or decreases slowly. This observation reveals that existing optimizers such as Adam, SGD, and RMSprop lack explicit mechanisms to exploit temporal consistency in gradient directions, relying instead on magnitude-based signals that fail to distinguish plateaus, saddle points, and genuine convergence. Motivated by this, we introduce GONO (Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer), which adapts Adam's momentum coefficient beta_1 based on cc_t: amplifying momentum under directional consistency and suppressing it during oscillation. We prove GONO matches Adam's O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence rate and reduces exactly to Adam when the signal is uninformative. Empirically, cc_t achieves oscillation detection with F1=1.00 (vs. 0.45 for gradient norm), and GONO remains competitive with AdamW on MNIST (98.15%), CIFAR-10 (43.14%), and ResNet-18 (75.44%), establishing directional alignment as a theoretically grounded, practically actionable optimization signal. Code: https://github.com/victordaniel/gono-optimizer

</details>


### [304] [On the Safety of Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06576)
*Xiaoguang Guo,Zehong Wang,Ziming Li,Shawn Spitzel,Soonwoo Kwon,Tianyi Ma,Yanfang Ye,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRL-Safety，一个多轴安全评估基准，用于系统评估图表示学习方法在五种安全维度（鲁棒性、OOD泛化、类别不平衡、公平性、可解释性）上的表现，涵盖12种代表性方法和25个图数据集，揭示了表征设计与压力因素的交互作用、基础模型时代方法的轴特异性优势以及现有方法的能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表示学习评估主要关注干净迁移、自适应和任务覆盖，忽略了部署压力（如图信号扰动、图上下文变化、标签支持不足、结构群体偏移、预测证据异常）对方法可靠性的影响，缺乏多维度安全性的系统评估。

Method: 构建GRL-Safety基准，在标准化评估条件下对12种代表性方法（包括拓扑嵌入、监督GNN、自监督图模型和图基础模型）在25个图数据集上进行评估，覆盖5个安全轴：腐败鲁棒性、OOD泛化、类别不平衡、公平性和可解释性，按轴和子条件报告而非单一聚合分数，同时保留方法原生自适应方式。

Result: 发现三个跨轴洞察：安全行为由表征设计与压力因素的交互决定而非方法家族；基础模型时代方法展现轴特异性优势而非全面安全主导；多个部署场景即使对最佳方法仍具挑战性，揭示需要新的鲁棒性、自适应或训练目标。

Conclusion: GRL-Safety为图表示学习的安全评估提供了标准化基准，识别了现有方法的能力缺口，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Graph representation learning (GRL) has evolved from topology-only graph embeddings to task-specific supervised GNNs, and more recently to reusable representations and graph foundation models (GFMs). However, existing evaluations mainly measure clean transfer, adaptation, and task coverage. It remains unclear whether GRL methods stay reliable when deployment stresses affect graph signals, graph contexts, label support, structural groups, or predictive evidence. We introduce GRL-Safety, a multi-axis safety evaluation benchmark for GRL. GRL-Safety evaluates twelve representative methods, spanning topology-only embedding methods, supervised GNNs, self-supervised graph models, and GFMs, on twenty-five graph datasets under standardized evaluation conditions while preserving method-native adaptation. The evaluation covers five safety axes: corruption robustness, OOD generalization, class imbalance, fairness, and interpretation, with per-axis and sub-condition reporting rather than a single aggregate score. Our analysis yields three cross-axis insights that can inspire future research. First, safety behavior is shaped by the interaction between representation design and the stressed graph factor, rather than by method family alone. Second, foundation-era methods show axis-specific strengths rather than broad safety dominance. Third, several deployment regimes remain difficult even for the best evaluated method, revealing capability gaps that require new robustness, adaptation, or training objectives beyond model selection. The benchmark, evaluation protocols, and code are available at: https://github.com/GXG-CS/GRL-Safety.

</details>


### [305] [Distributionally-Robust Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06585)
*Vinit Ranjan,Jisun Park,Bartolomeo Stellato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分布鲁棒方法用于学习凸优化中一阶算法的超参数，通过Wasserstein分布鲁棒性能估计问题统一了经典学习优化和最坏情况最优算法设计，在多个基准上取得优于两者的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习优化方法缺乏鲁棒性保证，而最坏情况最优设计过于保守，需要一种能平衡样本内性能与分布外鲁棒性的超参数学习方法。

Method: 构建Wasserstein分布鲁棒版本的性能估计问题(PEP)，通过随机梯度下降求解，每一步需对内部半正定规划进行微分。

Result: 在无约束二次优化、LASSO和线性规划基准上，学习到的算法在可证明鲁棒性下实现了强泛化性能，优于最坏情况最优和普通L2O基线。

Conclusion: 所提框架统一了L2O和最坏情况设计，提供了高概率风险界，证明真实风险不超过样本内L2O最优值加上随样本量衰减的松弛项，且不劣于最坏情况PEP界。

Abstract: We propose a distributionally robust approach to learning hyperparameters for first-order methods in convex optimization. Given a dataset of problem instances, we minimize a Wasserstein distributionally robust version of the performance estimation problem (PEP) over algorithm parameters such as step sizes. Our framework unifies two extremes: as the robustness radius vanishes, we recover classical learning to optimize (L2O); as it grows, we recover worst-case optimal algorithm design via PEP. We solve the resulting problem with stochastic gradient descent, differentiating through the solution of an inner semidefinite program at each step. We prove high-probability bounds showing that the true risk of the learned algorithm is at most the in-sample L2O optimum plus a slack that shrinks with the sample size, and is no worse than the worst-case PEP bound. On unconstrained quadratic minimization, LASSO, and linear programming benchmarks, our learned algorithms achieve strong out-of-sample performance with certifiable robustness, outperforming both worst-case optimal and vanilla L2O baselines.

</details>


### [306] [Weight-Decay Turns Transformer Loss Landscapes Villani: Functional-Analytic Foundations for Optimization and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06599)
*Abhijit Das,Sayantan Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从泛函分析角度严格刻画了带权重衰减（L2正则化）的Transformer损失函数，证明其满足Villani coercive能量函数准则，推导出log-Sobolev和Poincaré常数，并在GPT-Neo-125M上通过Hutchinson迹估计验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重衰减在大语言模型中广泛用作正则化器，但其在塑造Transformer损失景观中的精确作用在理论上尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 证明正则化后的交叉熵损失满足Villani coercive能量函数四准则：无穷可微、至少二次增长、高斯可积尾部、微分增长条件；2) 推导出log-Sobolev和Poincaré常数C_LS ≤ λ^{-1} + d/λ²；3) 提出可扩展的Villani诊断量Ψ_s(θ) = -ΔF + s^{-1}||∇F||²，并用Hutchinson迹估计高效计算。

Result: 在GPT-Neo-125M上，Penn Treebank和WikiText-103数据集验证了Ψ_s的二次增长、Hessian谱膨胀以及指数收敛行为，与log-Sobolev分析一致。

Conclusion: 权重衰减不仅能在经验上提升泛化性能，还从数学上建立了快速Langevin混合和曲率感知优化的理论基础。

Abstract: Weight decay is widely used as a regularizer in large language models, yet its precise role in shaping Transformer loss landscapes remains theoretically underexplored. This paper provides the first rigorous functional-analytic characterization of the standard Transformer objective--cross-entropy loss with $L^2$ regularization--by proving it satisfies Villani's criteria for coercive energy functions. Specifically, we show that the regularized loss $\mathcal{F}$ is infinitely differentiable, grows at least quadratically, has Gaussian-integrable tails, and satisfies the differential growth condition $-Δ\mathcal{F} + \tfrac{1}{s}\|\nabla\mathcal{F}\|^{2} \to \infty$ as $\|θ\| \to \infty$ for all $s>0$. From this structure, we derive explicit log-Sobolev and Poincaré constants $C_{\mathrm{LS}} \leq λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}$, linking the regularization strength $λ$ and model dimension $d$ to finite-time convergence guarantees for noisy stochastic gradient descent and PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds that tighten with increasing $λ$. To validate our theory, we introduce a scalable Villani diagnostic $Ψ_s(θ) = -Δ\mathcal{F} + s^{-1}\|\nabla \mathcal{F}\|^2$ and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson trace probes in models with over 100M parameters. Experiments on GPT-Neo-125M across Penn Treebank and WikiText-103 confirm the predicted quadratic growth of $Ψ_s$, spectral inflation of the Hessian, and exponential convergence behavior consistent with our log-Sobolev analysis. These results demonstrate that weight decay not only improves generalization empirically but also establishes the mathematical conditions required for fast Langevin mixing and theoretically grounded curvature-aware optimization in deep learning.

</details>


### [307] [How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06605)
*Shai Feldman,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DAPRO，首个理论上有效的动态预算分配框架，用于在多轮LLM交互中为事件发生时间构建可靠的置信下界。该框架满足预算约束、提供分布无关的有限样本覆盖保证，且覆盖界随平均删失权重的平方根而非最坏情况权重缩放，理论保证更紧。实验证明其在多种场景下优于静态基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话场景下LLM性能评估（如越狱、任务完成等关键事件）通常需要大量交互，但关键事件稀疏、计算开销大。现有共形生存框架采用静态预算分配，在多轮设置中效率低下，需要更高效的动态分配方法。

Method: 提出DAPRO（动态投影优化分配），一种动态预算分配框架，用于在多轮LLM交互中为时间-事件构建边界。该方法满足预算约束，提供分布无关的有限样本覆盖保证，且不要求删失与事件时间之间的条件独立性。理论贡献在于提出一种新的覆盖界，随平均删失权重的平方根缩放，比先前工作的最坏情况权重更紧。此外，DAPRO可用于在有限计算资源下获得无偏、低方差的人群级评估指标估计。

Result: 在多种LLM（Llama 3.1和Qwen 2.5）上，针对智能体任务成功、对抗性越狱、有毒内容生成和RAG幻觉等场景的综合实验表明，DAPRO在满足预算约束的同时，始终实现更接近名义水平的覆盖率和更低的方差，优于静态基线方法。

Conclusion: DAPRO是首个理论上有效的动态预算分配框架，在多轮LLM交互评估中显著提升效率与可靠性，具有重要的理论与实际应用价值。

Abstract: Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events -- e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent -- often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare, and under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved.
  Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that is inefficient in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce \emph{Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization} (DAPRO), the first theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions.
  We prove that DAPRO satisfies the budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches.
  A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight rather than the worst-case weight, yielding provably tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased, low-variance estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources.
  Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, adversarial jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations using LLMs such as Llama 3.1 and Qwen 2.5 demonstrate that DAPRO consistently achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while satisfying the budget constraint.

</details>


### [308] [Transformers Efficiently Perform In-Context Logistic Regression via Normalized Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06609)
*Chenyang Zhang,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Softmax Transformer如何在线性分类数据上实现上下文学习（ICL），构建了一类多层Transformer，每层精确执行一步归一化梯度下降，并证明该结构可通过单层自注意力训练和循环推理获得，同时提供了训练收敛和泛化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Transformer具有强大上下文学习能力的机制，特别是Softmax注意力如何隐式执行算法来增强预测和生成。

Method: (i) 构建多层Transformer，每层对应一步归一化梯度下降，用于上下文逻辑回归；(ii) 通过单层自注意力层的一步梯度下降监督训练获得该结构；(iii) 循环应用训练好的层得到循环模型；(iv) 提供训练收敛性和分布外泛化保证的理论证明。

Result: 成功构建了能够执行上下文逻辑回归的多层Softmax Transformer，并证明了通过单层自注意力训练加循环推理可得到该结构，同时给出了理论上的收敛与泛化保障。

Conclusion: 推进了对上下文学习机制的理论理解，展示了Softmax Transformer如何有效地作为上下文学习器工作。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities. The strong ICL performance of transformers is commonly believed to arise from their ability to implicitly execute certain algorithms on the context, thereby enhancing prediction and generation. In this work, we investigate how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification data. We first construct a class of multi-layer transformers that can perform in-context logistic regression, with each layer exactly performing one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. Then, we show that our constructed transformer can be obtained through (i) training a single self-attention layer supervised by one-step gradient descent, and (ii) recurrently applying the trained layer to obtain a looped model. Training convergence guarantees of the self-attention layer and out-of-distribution generalization guarantees of the looped model are provided. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of ICL mechanism by showcasing how softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners.

</details>


### [309] [The Structural Origin of Attention Sink: Variance Discrepancy, Super Neurons, and Dimension Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06611)
*Siquan Li,Kaiqi Jiang,Jiacheng Sun,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了注意力汇聚现象的机制成因：源于自注意力中的值聚合过程引起的方差差异，该差异被FFN层中超神经元的激活进一步放大，导致首个token需要形成注意力汇聚作为结构性锚点。通过两种干预实验验证了这一因果链，并提出head-wise RMSNorm这一架构改进来在预训练中稳定值聚合输出，加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽已注意到大语言模型中初始token过度占据注意力分数的"注意力汇聚"现象，但其结构起源尚不明确。本文旨在提供对该现象的机制性解释。

Method: 首先从理论上追溯注意力汇聚根源于自注意力中值聚合过程引发的系统性方差差异，并证明FFN层中超神经元的激活会放大这一差异。通过两种控制干预实验验证因果链：(i)通过注意力掩码修改隔离聚合效应；(ii)增强目标token表示的方差。最后提出head-wise RMSNorm架构改进。

Result: 两种干预实验均能在任意位置复制注意力汇聚现象。提出的head-wise RMSNorm在预训练中能够恢复各位置间的统计平衡，显著加速收敛。

Conclusion: 本文提供了注意力汇聚现象的机制性解释，揭示了其与值聚合过程及FFN层超神经元激活的因果联系，并为系统性控制汇聚形成奠定了基础。head-wise RMSNorm作为概念验证，展示了通过恢复统计平衡改善训练效率的可行性。

Abstract: Despite the prevalence of the attention sink phenomenon in Large Language Models (LLMs), where initial tokens disproportionately monopolize attention scores, its structural origins remain elusive. This work provides a \textit{mechanistic explanation} for this phenomenon. First, we trace its root to the value aggregation process inherent in self-attention, which induces a systematic variance discrepancy. We further demonstrate that this discrepancy is drastically amplified by the activation of super neurons within Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layers. Specifically, the channel-sparse down-projections trigger a dimension disparity of the first-token representation, necessitating the formation of attention sinks as a structural anchor. Then, we validate this causal chain through two controlled interventions: (i) isolating the aggregation effect via attention mask modifications and (ii) amplifying the variance of targeted token representations. Both interventions can replicate attention sinks at arbitrary positions. Our mechanistic understanding offers a foundation for the systematic control of sink formation. Finally, as a proof of concept, we propose \textit{head-wise RMSNorm}, an architectural modification that stabilizes value aggregation outputs during pre-training. Our experiments demonstrate that restoring statistical parity across positions significantly accelerates convergence.

</details>


### [310] [Online Bayesian Calibration under Gradual and Abrupt System Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06612)
*Yang Xu,Chiwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在线贝叶斯校准框架BRPC，通过分离粒子更新和高斯过程更新解决参数-偏差混杂问题，并集成重启机制应对系统突变，适用于流式数据和非平稳环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯校准存在参数-偏差混杂、离线静态假设等局限，无法适应数字孪生中系统随时间的渐变漂移和突变。

Method: BRPC将投影校准扩展至在线设置：用无偏差的粒子更新校准参数，用条件高斯过程更新偏差函数，保持可辨识性；集成重启机制检测突变并重置校准。

Result: 在合成数据和植物仿真基准上，BRPC在渐变下校准精度更优，重启增强版在突变下鲁棒性和预测性能优于滑动窗口贝叶斯校准和数据同化基线。

Conclusion: BRPC为数字孪生中的在线偏差感知校准提供了理论保证和有效方法，解决了渐变和突变场景下的关键挑战。

Abstract: Bayesian model calibration is central to digital twins and computer experiments, as it aligns model outputs with field observations by estimating calibration parameters and correcting systematic model bias. Classical Bayesian calibration introduces latent parameters and a discrepancy function to model bias, but suffers from parameter--discrepancy confounding and is typically formulated as an offline procedure under a stationary data-generating assumption. These limitations are restrictive in modern digital twin applications, where systems evolve over time and may exhibit gradual drift and abrupt regime shifts. While data assimilation methods enable sequential updates, they generally do not explicitly model systematic bias and are less effective under abrupt changes. We propose Bayesian Recursive Projected Calibration (BRPC), an online Bayesian calibration framework for streaming data under simulator mismatch and nonstationarity. BRPC extends projected calibration to the online setting by separating a discrepancy-free particle update for calibration parameters from a conditional Gaussian process update for discrepancy, preserving identifiability while enabling bias-aware adaptation under gradual system evolution. To handle abrupt changes, BRPC is integrated with restart mechanisms that detect regime shifts and reset the calibration process. We establish theoretical guarantees for both components, including tracking performance under gradual evolution and false-alarm and detection behavior for restart mechanisms. Empirical studies on synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks show that BRPC improves calibration accuracy under gradual changes, while restart-augmented BRPC further improves robustness and predictive performance under abrupt regime shifts compared to sliding-window Bayesian calibration and data assimilation baselines.

</details>


### [311] [Concept-Based Abductive and Contrastive Explanations for Behaviors of Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06640)
*Ronaldo Canizales,Divya Gopinath,Corina Păsăreanu,Ravi Mangal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于概念的反事实解释和溯因解释，通过概念擦除技术建立因果关系，计算导致模型输出的最小高层概念集合，支持单个图像和图像集合的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于概念的解释方法要么缺乏因果关系，要么只能处理单一概念；同时，形式溯因/反事实解释只关注像素级低层特征。本文旨在融合这两条研究路线，提出因果相关的高层概念级最小解释。

Method: 定义概念级溯因和反事实解释，设计枚举所有最小解释的算法族，采用概念擦除过程建立因果关系，并通过聚合解释支持对模型在图像集合上特定行为的理解。

Result: 在多个模型、数据集和行为上验证了方法的有效性，能够生成有用且用户友好的解释。

Conclusion: 概念级溯因/反事实解释融合了概念可解释性与形式化因果解释的优势，为理解模型在个体和集体层面的决策行为提供了更强大、更可解释的工具。

Abstract: *Concept-based explanations* offer a promising approach for explaining the predictions of deep neural networks in terms of high-level, human-understandable concepts. However, existing methods either do not establish a causal connection between the concepts and model predictions or are limited in expressivity and only able to infer causal explanations involving single concepts. At the same time, the parallel line of work on *formal abductive and contrastive explanations* computes the minimal set of input features causally relevant for model outcomes but only considers low-level features such as pixels. Merging these two threads, in this work, we propose the notion of *concept-based abductive and contrastive explanations* that capture the minimal sets of high-level concepts causally relevant for model outcomes. We then present a family of algorithms that enumerate all minimal explanations while using *concept erasure* procedures to establish causal relationships. By appropriately aggregating such explanations, we are not only able to understand model predictions on individual images but also on collections of images where the model exhibits a user-specified, common *behavior*. We evaluate our approach on multiple models, datasets, and behaviors, and demonstrate its effectiveness in computing helpful, user-friendly explanations.

</details>


### [312] [Inductive Venn-Abers and related regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06646)
*Ivan Petej,Vladimir Vovk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Venn-Abers预测器从二分类和有界回归扩展到无界回归，结合共形预测元素，实验表明该方法在大训练集上能略微提升标准回归器的预测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Venn-Abers预测器具有有效的概率性质，但仅适用于二分类和有界回归场景，限制了其在更广泛回归问题中的应用。本文旨在将其推广到无界回归场景。

Method: 通过引入共形预测的元素，将Venn-Abers预测器从有界回归扩展到无界回归情形，并基于Venn-Abers回归器推导点回归器。

Result: 模拟和实证研究表明，基于Venn-Abers回归器的点回归器在较大训练集上相比标准回归器具有略微提升的预测效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的无界回归Venn-Abers方法保留了有效性属性，并在大训练样本下展现出改进预测效率的潜力。

Abstract: Venn-Abers predictors are probabilistic predictors that enjoy appealing properties of validity, but their major limitation is that they are applicable only to the case of binary classification, with a recent extension to bounded regression. We generalize them to the case of unbounded regression, which requires adding an element of conformal prediction. In our simulation and empirical studies we investigate the predictive efficiency of point regressors derived from Venn-Abers regressors and argue that they somewhat improve the predictive efficiency of standard regressors for larger training sets.

</details>


### [313] [Optimizer-Model Consistency: Full Finetuning with the Same Optimizer as Pretraining Forgets Less](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06654)
*Yuxing Liu,Jianyu Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现监督微调（SFT）阶段使用与预训练相同的优化器（即优化器-模型一致性）能在学习-遗忘权衡中表现更优，比使用其他优化器甚至LoRA遗忘更少且性能相当或更好。通过实验和理论分析，优化器通过对激活的正则化效应塑造模型，而SFT中的权重更新需遵循特定结构以降低遗忘。此外，对比Muon和AdamW发现，Muon在推理任务微调中表现更差，因其倾向于死记硬背，不利于小样本下的模式习得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在LLM的监督微调阶段，使用与预训练相同的优化器是否能在学习新任务的同时更好地保留预训练知识，即优化器-模型一致性现象。

Method: 通过控制实验和理论分析，研究优化器对激活的正则化效应及其对模型损失景观的影响；对比Muon和AdamW在预训练和微调全流程中的表现；设计合成语言建模实验分析Muon的死记硬背倾向。

Result: 相同优化器在SFT中遗忘更少且性能不降；优化器通过正则化效应塑造模型损失景观；Muon在推理任务微调中表现更差，因其强死记硬背倾向不利于小样本模式习得。

Conclusion: 优化器-模型一致性是SFT阶段提升学习-遗忘权衡的关键因素，不同优化器的内在正则化特性会影响微调效果，建议SFT中使用与预训练一致的优化器。

Abstract: Optimizers play an important role in both pretraining and finetuning stages when training large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we present an observation that full finetuning with the same optimizer as in pretraining achieves a better learning-forgetting tradeoff, i.e., forgetting less while achieving the same or better performance on the new task, than other optimizers and, possibly surprisingly, LoRA, during the supervised finetuning (SFT) stage. We term this phenomenon optimizer-model consistency. To better understand it, through controlled experiments and theoretical analysis, we show that: 1) optimizers can shape the models by having regularization effects on the activations, leading to different landscapes around the pretrained checkpoints; 2) in response to this regularization effect, the weight update in SFT should follow some specific structures to lower forgetting of the knowledge learned in pretraining, which can be obtained by using the same optimizer. Moreover, we specifically compare Muon and AdamW when they are employed throughout the pretraining and SFT stages and find that Muon performs worse when finetuned for reasoning tasks. With a synthetic language modeling experiment, we demonstrate that this can come from Muon's strong tendency towards rote memorization, which may hurt pattern acquisition with a small amount of data, as for SFT.

</details>


### [314] [UniPool: A Globally Shared Expert Pool for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06665)
*Minbin Huang,Han Shi,Chuanyang Zheng,Yimeng Wu,Guoxuan Chen,Xintong Yu,Yichun Yin,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UniPool架构，用共享专家池替代每层独立专家集，使专家参数可随深度亚线性增长，在多个规模上优于传统MoE，且仅用41.6%-66.7%的专家参数量即可匹配或超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE每层独立分配专家集，导致深度扩展时专家参数线性增长，且研究发现深层路由存在冗余（随机路由仅降1.0-1.6个点）。

Method: 用全局共享专家池替代逐层专家所有权，每层独立路由器从池中选择专家；引入池级辅助损失平衡专家利用率，采用NormRouter实现稀疏稳定路由。

Result: 在五个规模（182M-978M参数）上训练30B tokens，UniPool持续降低验证损失（最高降0.0386）；缩减池版本仅用41.6%-66.7%专家参数即匹配或超越基线。

Conclusion: 共享池设计下专家参数无需随深度线性增长，可实现亚线性增长且更高效；池大小可作为显式的深度缩放超参数。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures allocate expert capacity through a rigid per-layer rule: each transformer layer owns a separate expert set. This convention couples depth scaling with linear expert-parameter growth and assumes that every layer needs isolated expert capacity. However, recent analyses and our routing probe challenge this allocation rule: replacing a deeper layer's learned top-k router with uniform random routing drops downstream accuracy by only 1.0-1.6 points across multiple production MoE models. Motivated by this redundancy, we propose UniPool, an MoE architecture that treats expert capacity as a global architectural budget by replacing per-layer expert ownership with a single shared pool accessed by independent per-layer routers. To enable stable and balanced training under sharing, we introduce a pool-level auxiliary loss that balances expert utilization across the entire pool, and adopt NormRouter to provide sparse and scale-stable routing into the shared expert pool. Across five LLaMA-architecture model scales (182M, 469M, 650M, 830M, and 978M parameters) trained on 30B tokens from the Pile, UniPool consistently improves validation loss and perplexity over the matched vanilla MoE baselines. Across these scales, UniPool reduces validation loss by up to 0.0386 relative to vanilla MoE. Beyond raw loss improvement, our results identify pool size as an explicit depth-scaling hyperparameter: reduced-pool UniPool variants using only 41.6%-66.7% of the vanilla expert-parameter budget match or outperform layer-wise MoE at the tested scales. This shows that, under a shared-pool design, expert parameters need not grow linearly with depth; they can grow sublinearly while remaining more efficient and effective than vanilla MoE. Further analysis shows that UniPool's benefits compose with finer-grained expert decomposition.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [315] [Dynamic Graph with Similarity-Aware Attention Graph Neural Network for Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05238)
*Aadarsh Senapati,Neha Kujur,Vivek Yelleti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-SA-GNN框架，通过动态用户相似图构建、多相似度传播和注意力聚合，在MovieLens100K上Recall@20达0.162，优于LightGCN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统协同过滤依赖静态交互图和有限相似度度量，无法捕捉用户偏好的动态变化；现有GNN方法未显式建模用户-用户关系及动态图演化。

Method: 构建四种并行用户相似图（Cosine、Jaccard、Discount PCC、IPIJ），经专用UserGNN处理；Graph Transformer融合四视图，CrossAttention与物品嵌入交互；训练中按调度周期重构图，实现动态图组件；采用小批量训练与硬负样本采样。

Result: 在MovieLens100K上，Recall@20达0.162，NDCG@20达0.065，召回率优于LightGCN基线。

Conclusion: 动态多相似度图构建与注意力融合能有效提升推荐性能，验证了动态图演化对捕捉用户偏好变化的重要性。

Abstract: Recommender systems are essential components of modern online platforms which presents personalized content in various domain. The traditional collaborative filtering methods depends on static user-item interaction graphs and a limited subset of similarity measures which fail to capture the changing nature of preferences of an individual. Recent graph neural network (GNN) based approaches focus on user-item bipartite graphs which do not use explicit user-user relational modelling and dynamic graph evolution during training. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a Dynamic Graph SimilarityAware Attention Graph Neural Network (DG-SA-GNN) framework that integrates dynamic user similarity graph construction with multi-similarity propagation and attention-based aggregation. The proposed architecture constructs four parallel user similarity graphs using Cosine, Jaccard, Discounted Pearson Correlation Coefficient (Discount PCC), and IPIJ similarity functions, each processed by a dedicated UserGNN module. A Graph Transformer fuses the four graph views, and a CrossAttention module refines user embeddings through interaction with item embeddings. Crucially, the graphs are reconstructed at scheduled epochs during training, enabling the model to adapt to the learned embedding space constituting the dynamic graph component. Mini-batch training with hard negative sampling improves scalability and convergence. Experiments on the MovieLens100K benchmark demonstrate that DG-SA-GNN achieves a Recall@20 of 0.162 and NDCG@20 of 0.065 which is better than the LightGCN baseline in recall. The results validate that dynamic multi-similarity graph construction coupled with attention-based fusion which produce recommendation performance

</details>


### [316] [Beyond Semantic Similarity: Rethinking Retrieval for Agentic Search via Direct Corpus Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05242)
*Zhuofeng Li,Haoxiang Zhang,Cong Wei,Pan Lu,Ping Nie,Yi Lu,Yuyang Bai,Shangbin Feng,Hangxiao Zhu,Ming Zhong,Yuyu Zhang,Jianwen Xie,Yejin Choi,James Zou,Jiawei Han,Wenhu Chen,Jimmy Lin,Dongfu Jiang,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出直接语料交互（DCI），让智能体通过grep、文件读取等通用终端工具直接操作原始语料，无需嵌入模型、向量索引或检索API。在多个IR基准和端到端智能体搜索任务中，DCI显著优于传统稀疏、稠密和重排序基线，表明检索质量不仅取决于推理能力，还取决于模型与语料交互的接口分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索系统（词法或语义）通过固定相似度接口将语料访问压缩为单步top-k检索，这对于智能体搜索成为瓶颈：精确词法约束、稀疏线索组合、局部上下文检查及多步假设优化难以实现，且早期过滤的证据无法被下游推理恢复。智能体任务需要多步编排，包括发现中间实体、组合弱线索、根据部分证据调整计划，传统检索器无法满足这些需求。

Method: DCI（Direct Corpus Interaction）方法：智能体直接通过通用终端工具（如grep、文件读取、shell命令、轻量级脚本）与原始语料交互，无需任何嵌入模型、向量索引或检索API，也不需要离线索引，能自然适应动态演化的本地语料。

Result: 在BRIGHT和BEIR等多个数据集上，DCI显著优于强稀疏（如BM25）、稠密（如Contriever）和重排序基线；在BrowseComp-Plus和多跳QA任务上取得高准确率，且不依赖任何传统语义检索器。

Conclusion: 随着语言智能体能力的增强，检索质量不仅取决于推理能力，还取决于模型与语料交互的接口分辨率。DCI为智能体搜索开辟了更广阔的接口设计空间。

Abstract: Modern retrieval systems, whether lexical or semantic, expose a corpus through a fixed similarity interface that compresses access into a single top-k retrieval step before reasoning. This abstraction is efficient, but for agentic search, it becomes a bottleneck: exact lexical constraints, sparse clue conjunctions, local context checks, and multi-step hypothesis refinement are difficult to implement by calling a conventional off-the-shelf retriever, and evidence filtered out early cannot be recovered by stronger downstream reasoning. Agentic tasks further exacerbate this limitation because they require agents to orchestrate multiple steps, including discovering intermediate entities, combining weak clues, and revising the plan after observing partial evidence. To tackle the limitation, we study direct corpus interaction (DCI), where an agent searches the raw corpus directly with general-purpose terminal tools (e.g., grep, file reads, shell commands, lightweight scripts), without any embedding model, vector index, or retrieval API. This approach requires no offline indexing and adapts naturally to evolving local corpora. Across IR benchmarks and end-to-end agentic search tasks, this simple setup substantially outperforms strong sparse, dense, and reranking baselines on several BRIGHT and BEIR datasets, and attains strong accuracy on BrowseComp-Plus and multi-hop QA without relying on any conventional semantic retriever. Our results indicate that as language agents become stronger, retrieval quality depends not only on reasoning ability but also on the resolution of the interface through which the model interacts with the corpus, with which DCI opens a broader interface-design space for agentic search.

</details>


### [317] [Towards Dependable Retrieval-Augmented Generation Using Factual Confidence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05244)
*Florian Geissler,Francesco Carella,Laura Fieback,Jakob Spiegelberg*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段方法，用于预测检索增强生成（RAG）输出的事实忠实度：第一阶段使用保形预测筛选高相关性的检索块；第二阶段使用基于注意力的事实验证分类器量化生成答案与检索上下文的一致性。同时提供了诊断指标来评估保形预测的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG技术广泛用于将特定知识注入大语言模型，但检索到的上下文可能包含不相关或误导性信息，影响生成答案的事实性。现有方法缺乏对检索过程事实性的有效置信度度量，亟需一种可靠的机制来评估RAG输出的忠实度。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) 保形预测阶段——筛选检索块中正确来源概率高的子集，提升答案质量最高达6%；同时提出诊断指标评估样本可交换性假设是否成立。(2) 基于注意力的事实验证分类器——量化生成答案与给定检索上下文之间的一致性，能检测高达77%的不一致答案。

Result: 第一阶段可将答案质量提升最高6%；第二阶段可检测高达77%的不一致答案。但保形预测的统计保证在非可交换性设置下不普遍成立。

Conclusion: 本文为建立经过认证的RAG系统奠定了新基础，适用于广泛的自然语言行业应用场景。

Abstract: Incorporating specific knowledge into large language models via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a widespread technique that fuels many of today's industry AI applications. A fundamental problem is to assess if the context retrieved by some similarity search provides indeed supporting facts, or instead misguides the generator with irrelevant information. It is critical to associate meaningful confidence measures about the factuality of the retrieval process with the generated answers. We present a new, two-staged approach to predict fact faithfulness of the output of retrieval-augmented generations. First, we employ conformal prediction to select only those retrieved chunks who have a high chance to come from the correct source. This approach in itself can improve answer quality by up to 6% in some of the studied datasets, however, the associated statistical guarantees do not hold generally, since the assumption of sample exchangeability depends on the retriever setup. We present diagnostic metrics to assess whether a setup is suitable. Second, we quantify confidence in the consistency of a generated final answer with a given retrieved context, using an attention-based factuality classifier. This approach can detect inconsistent answers with a chance of up to 77%. Our work helps to establish a novel type of certified RAG systems for a broad range of natural language industry applications.

</details>


### [318] [TriAlignGR: Triangular Multitask Alignment with Multimodal Deep Interest Mining for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05249)
*Yangchen Zeng,Hao Peng,Rongfeng Guo,Zhenyu Yu,Zhiyuan Hu,Jinze Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TriAlignGR提出统一的多任务多模态生成式推荐框架，通过两阶段语义传播（视觉编码到SID+视觉描述解码）解决SID内容退化（SCD）和语义不透明（SSO）问题，核心包括跨模态对齐、深度兴趣挖掘和三角多任务训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SID管道存在两个根本问题：SCD（级联编码和残差量化丢弃多模态和兴趣级语义）和SSO（模型自回归生成SID序列但不理解其含义，导致幻觉和泛化差）。先前工作最多只做文本-SID对齐，完全未利用视觉语义和潜在用户兴趣。

Method: 三部分集成：(1) CMSA：通过VLM生成文本描述+多模态嵌入模型直接编码图像特征，将视觉内容融入SID构建；(2) MDIM：利用LLM链式思维推理从视觉内容中提取潜在用户意图，丰富SID语义；(3) TMT：在单一自回归损失下联合训练八个互补生成任务（含两个新视觉语义任务VisDesc→SID和VisDesc→Title），构建SID-文本-图像三角。

Result: 通过两阶段多模态语义传播（编码视觉语义到SID+通过视觉描述任务解码语义），解决了SCD和SSO问题，无需特定任务塔或复杂损失加权。

Conclusion: TriAlignGR是首个统一解决SID中多模态语义丢失和语义不透明问题的框架，通过三角多任务学习实现SID-文本-图像的完整语义对齐，提升生成式推荐的泛化能力。

Abstract: We introduce TriAlignGR, a unified multitask-multimodal framework for generative recommendation that establishes two-stage multimodal semantic propagation: (i) encoding visual semantics directly into SIDs via multimodal embeddings, and (ii) enabling the model to decode these semantics through visual description tasks. Existing Semantic ID (SID) pipelines suffer from two fundamental but underexplored problems: \textbf{SID Content Degradation (SCD)}, where cascaded encoding and residual quantization discard critical multimodal and interest-level semantics; and \textbf{SID Semantic Opacity (SSO)}, where models autoregressively generate SID sequences without truly comprehending their underlying meaning, leading to hallucination and poor generalization. Prior work addresses at most text-SID alignment, leaving visual semantics and latent user interests entirely unexploited. TriAlignGR resolves both problems through three tightly integrated components: (1)~\textbf{Cross-Modal Semantic Alignment (CMSA)} integrates visual content into SID construction through both VLM-generated textual descriptions and a multimodal embedding model that directly encodes image features alongside text, ensuring that SIDs inherently carry multimodal semantics; (2)~\textbf{Multimodal Deep Interest Mining (MDIM)} leverages LLM Chain-of-Thought reasoning to extract latent user intents (\eg ``productivity-focused lifestyle'' from noise-canceling headphones) beyond surface attributes, enriching SID semantics before discretization; and (3)~\textbf{Triangular Multitask (TMT)} jointly trains on eight complementary generation tasks under a single autoregressive loss -- including two novel visual-semantic tasks (VisDesc$\to$SID, VisDesc$\to$Title) that map VLM-generated image descriptions to SIDs and titles, completing the SID-Text-Image triangle -- without requiring task-specific towers or complex loss weighting.

</details>


### [319] [Decision-aware User Simulation Agent for Evaluating Conversational Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05250)
*Yuan-Chi Li,Li-Chi Chen,Sung-Yi Wu,Yu-Che Tsai,Shou-De Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对对话推荐系统中用户模拟器过度接受推荐、缺乏真实决策过程建模的问题，提出Hesitator框架，通过模块化决策模块分离效用选择与过载感知承诺决策，在多种设置下有效缓解不现实行为并复现心理学经济学行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟器，包括基于LLM的模拟器，表现出不切实际的强信息处理能力，很少展现出真实消费者中常见的犹豫或延迟决策行为，导致推荐接受概率过高。需要一种理论驱动的框架来显式建模人类在信息过载下的决策过程。

Method: 提出Hesitator框架，包含模块化决策模块，将决策过程分离为两个子过程：(1) 基于效用的项目选择；(2) 过载感知的承诺决策。该模块可集成到现有用户模拟框架中，支持多种销售模式和LLM后端。

Result: 在多种用户模拟框架、领域、销售模式和LLM骨干上的实验表明，集成Hesitator模块能持续缓解过载条件下的不现实行为。同时，该框架成功复现了心理学经济学中的已知行为模式。

Conclusion: Hesitator提供了理论扎实的用户模拟方案，通过显式建模选择过载下的决策过程，显著提升了对话推荐系统中用户模拟的行为真实性。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems (CRS) increasingly rely on user simulators for automated evaluation of sales agents. A key requirement for such simulators is the ability to model human decision-making. However, most existing simulation frameworks do not explicitly model the internal decision process, and LLM-based simulators often exhibit unrealistically strong information-processing capabilities, rarely exhibit the hesitation or decision deferral commonly observed in real consumer behavior, resulting in overly high acceptance probabilities. To address this limitation, we propose Hesitator, a theory-grounded user simulation framework that explicitly models human decision-making under choice overload. The framework introduces a modular Decision Module that separates utility-based item selection from overload-aware commitment decisions. Experiments across multiple user simulation frameworks, domains, sales modes, and LLM backbones show that integrating our module consistently mitigates unrealistic behaviors under increasing overload conditions. Furthermore, Hesitator reproduces established behavioral patterns from psychological economics, demonstrating its ability to model human decision behavior.

</details>


### [320] [Career-Aware Resume Tailoring via Multi-Source Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Provenance Tracking: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05257)
*Kumar Abhinav*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Resume Tailor，一个基于纵向职业库和多源RAG的智能简历定制系统，通过12节点LangGraph流水线实现简历优化。实验表明，当存在相关历史经验时，ATS匹配分数平均提升7.8分；当领域不匹配时，分数下降8.0分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助简历系统仅基于单次上传的简历进行操作，无法恢复当前草稿中遗漏的相关经验，且用户难以区分基于事实的编辑与模型生成的建议。

Method: 构建纵向职业向量数据库，采用多源检索增强生成（RAG）从历史简历和结构化职业记录中组装岗位定制内容；实现为12节点LangGraph流水线，包含类型化状态管理、混合语义-词汇置信度评分、溯源回退生成、反幻觉护栏及条件性审查循环。

Result: 在9个JD上评估：6个相关岗位平均提升7.8分ATS分数；2个跨领域岗位平均下降8.0分；1个部分重叠岗位提升2分。

Conclusion: 纵向检索在存在相关过往经验时可提升简历定制效果，同时需要置信度门控检索机制以应对领域重叠不足的情况。

Abstract: AI-assisted resume tailoring systems commonly operate on a single uploaded resume, which limits their ability to recover relevant experience omitted from the current draft and makes it difficult for users to distinguish grounded edits from model-generated suggestions. This paper presents Resume Tailor, an agentic resume-tailoring system that maintains a longitudinal career vault in a vector database and uses multi-source retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to assemble job-specific resume content from historical resumes and structured career records. The system is implemented as a 12-node LangGraph pipeline with typed state management, hybrid semantic-lexical confidence scoring, provenance-aware fallback generation, anti-hallucination guardrails, and a conditional review loop. We report a pilot evaluation on nine job descriptions (JDs) across software engineering, data analytics, and business analysis roles using a single candidate's career history. For six JDs where the candidate held at least one prior role in the same occupational category, enabling the career vault improved Applicant Tracking System (ATS)-style fit scores by an average of 7.8 points. For two JDs requiring domain-specific expertise absent from the vault, scores decreased by an average of 8.0 points. One partially overlapping role showed a modest gain of 2 points. These results suggest that longitudinal retrieval can improve resume tailoring when relevant prior experience exists, while also highlighting the need for confidence-gated retrieval when domain overlap is weak.

</details>


### [321] [Effective Knowledge Transfer for Multi-Task Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05730)
*Guohao Cai,Jun Yuan,Zhenhua Dong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出EKTM方法，通过路由模块和发射器模块实现多任务推荐模型中的知识迁移，以解决CVR预测中用户转化行为稀疏的问题，在基准数据集和工业场景中均取得SOTA效果，eCPM提升3.93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVR是衡量平台效果的关键指标，但用户转化行为稀疏导致排序模型训练困难，亟需有效利用多任务间的知识迁移来提升模型性能。

Method: 提出EKTM方法，包含三个核心模块：(1) 路由模块：整合并在任务间传播知识；(2) 发射器模块：为每个CVR任务配备，将路由中的知识进行转换；(3) 增强模块：确保迁移的知识有利于原任务学习。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上超越现有SOTA方法；在商业平台在线A/B测试中eCPM提升3.93%，已全面部署至两个主要流量场景。

Conclusion: EKTM通过多任务知识迁移有效缓解了CVR稀疏问题，在学术界和工业界均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: The conversion rate (CVR) is a crucial metric for evaluating the effectiveness of platforms, as it quantifies the alignment of content with audience preferences. However, the limited nature of customers' conversion actions presents a significant challenge for training ranking models effectively. In this paper, we propose an Effective Knowledge Transfer method for Multi-task Recommendation Models (EKTM). This method enables the ranking model to learn from diverse user behaviors, thereby enhancing performance through the transfer of knowledge across distinct yet related tasks. Each specific CVR task can directly benefit from the insights provided by other tasks. To achieve this, we first introduce a router module that integrates and disseminates knowledge across tasks. Subsequently, each CVR task is equipped with a transmitter module that facilitates the transformation of knowledge from the router. Additionally, we propose an enhanced module to ensure that the transferred knowledge benefit the original task learning. Extensive experiments on several benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed method outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches. Online A/B testing on a commercial platform has validated the effectiveness of the EKTM algorithm in large-scale industrial settings, resulting in a 3.93% uplift in effective Cost Per Mille (eCPM). The algorithm has since been fully deployed across two of the platform's main-traffic scenarios.

</details>


### [322] [Beyond Long Tail POIs: Transition-Centered Generalization for Human Mobility Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05771)
*Dingyang Lyu,Zhengjia Xu,Jey Han Lau,Jianzhong Qi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文发现人类移动性预测的核心瓶颈在于**过渡级长尾泛化**（即源-目的地转换对在训练集中极少出现），并提出RECAP框架，通过**多跳传递性**和**历史重访证据**重构长尾过渡，结合**暖过渡留出训练**抑制记忆、鼓励泛化，在多个真实数据集上显著提升预测精度，特别是对尾过渡的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注POI级别的长尾问题，但作者发现即使对热门POI的访问也经常预测失败，根本原因是**过渡级稀疏性**——对应的源-目的地转换对在训练集中罕见或从未出现。因此需要解决过渡级长尾泛化问题。

Method: 提出RECAP框架：1）**过渡重构**——从全局过渡图的**多跳传递性**和用户历史轨迹的**重访证据**两种可泛化信号中重构长尾过渡；2）**暖过渡留出训练**——抑制对频繁过渡的记忆，鼓励从可迁移信号中泛化。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上，RECAP一致地提高了预测准确性，对尾过渡的预测效果提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 人类移动性预测的核心瓶颈在过渡级长尾泛化，而非POI级长尾；通过重构过渡和针对性训练策略可有效解决此问题。

Abstract: Human mobility prediction forecasts a user's next Point of Interest (POI) from historical trajectories, supporting applications from recommendation to urban planning. Recent studies have recognized the problem with long-tail POIs in human mobility prediction, which are POIs with few visit records, making new visits to such POIs difficult to predict. Our analysis shows that many predictions fail even for visits to popular POIs. The underlying cause is often transition-level sparsity: the corresponding source-destination transition appears rarely, or never appears, in the training set. We therefore argue that a core bottleneck in human mobility prediction lies in transition-level long-tail generalization. We formulate this problem as compositional generalization and propose a tRansition rEconstruction framework for Compositional generAlization in next-POI prediction (RECAP). RECAP reconstructs long-tail transitions from two generalizable signals: multi-hop transitivity in the global transition graph and revisit evidence from a user's historical trajectory. It further uses warm-transition holdout training to discourage memorization of frequent transitions and encourage generalization from transferable signals. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets show that RECAP consistently improves prediction accuracy, with clear gains on tail transitions.

</details>


### [323] [Unified Value Alignment for Generative Recommendation in Industrial Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05803)
*Xinxun Zhang,Yuling Xiong,Jiale Zhou,Zhengkai Guo,Zhennan Pang,Junbang Huo,Jingwen Wang,Xuyang Sun,Enming Zhang,Jiaguang Jin,Changping Wang,Yi Li,Jun Zhang,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出UniVA框架，通过商业SID分词器、生成即排序的解码器、价值引导的波束搜索三步，将商业价值信号对齐到生成式推荐的全流程中，在腾讯广告平台取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐（GR）在推荐场景表现良好，但将其扩展到工业广告场景时面临挑战，因为广告系统不仅需优化用户兴趣，还需优化商业价值。现有GR管线以语义为中心，难以将价值信号对齐到分词、解码和在线服务等环节。

Method: UniVA包含三个核心组件：1) 商业SID分词器，将价值相关属性注入SID构建中，产生价值可区分的物品表示；2) 生成即排序SID解码器，通过监督学习和eCPM感知的强化学习联合优化，在解码过程中融合价值分数以同时完成生成和排序；3) 价值引导的个性化波束搜索，复用解码logits作为在线价值引导，并使用个性化trie树约束解码路径。

Result: 在腾讯微信视频号广告平台上，UniVA离线Hit Rate@100提升37.04%，在线A/B测试GMV提升1.5%。

Conclusion: UniVA通过在GR流程中系统性地对齐商业价值信号，有效解决了生成式推荐在工业广告场景中的价值优化难题，验证了将商业价值感知引入生成式广告推荐的可行性。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) reformulates recommendation as a next-token generation problem and has shown promise in industrial applications. However, extending GR to industrial advertising is non-trivial because the system must optimize not only user interest but also commercial value. Existing GR pipelines remain largely semantics-centric, making it difficult to align value signals across tokenization, decoding, and online serving. To address this issue, we propose UniVA, a Unified Value Alignment framework for advertising recommendation. We first introduce a Commercial SID tokenizer that injects value-related attributes into SID construction, yielding value-discriminative item representations. We then develop a Generation-as-Ranking SID Decoder jointly optimized by supervised learning and eCPM-aware reinforcement learning, which fuses value scores into next-item SID generation to perform generation and ranking in one decoding process. Finally, we design a value-guided personalized beam search that reuses generation-as-ranking logits as online value guidance and applies a personalized trie tree to constrain decoding to request-valid SID paths. Experiments on the Tencent WeChat Channels advertising platform show that UniVA achieves a 37.04\% improvement in offline Hit Rate@100 over the baseline and a 1.5\% GMV lift in online A/B tests.

</details>


### [324] [Bridging Passive and Active: Enhancing Conversation Starter Recommendation via Active Expression Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05855)
*Yiqing Wu,Haoming Li,Guanyu Jiang,Jiahao Liang,Yongchun Zhu,Jingwu Chen,Feng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM驱动对话搜索中推荐启动器的"曝光-点击"反馈循环导致的回声室问题，提出PA-Bridge框架，通过对抗分布对齐器桥接被动推荐与主动表达之间的分布差异，并引入语义离散化器实现流行度去偏，在线A/B测试显示功能渗透率提升0.54%，用户活跃天数提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话搜索推荐启动器依赖"曝光-点击"闭环，导致系统陷入回声室，偏向流行但通用的建议，无法捕捉开放世界中用户动态的对话搜索意图。

Method: 提出PA-Bridge框架，包含对抗分布对齐器（Adversarial Distribution Aligner）桥接被动推荐启动器与主动用户表达之间的分布差距，以及语义离散化器（Semantic Discretizer）实现大规模工业流式训练中的流行度去偏算法。

Result: 在线A/B测试中，PA-Bridge显著提升了功能渗透率（Feature Penetration Rate）0.54%和用户活跃天数（User Active Days）。

Conclusion: 利用用户主动查询中的开放世界意图可以有效打破传统推荐系统的反馈循环，PA-Bridge通过分布对齐和语义离散化成功实现了这一范式转变。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-driven conversational search is shifting information retrieval from reactive keyword matching to proactive, open-ended dialogues. In this context, Conversation Starters are widely deployed to provide personalized query recommendations that help users initiate dialogues. Conventionally, recommending these starters relies on a closed "exposure-click" loop. Yet, this feedback loop mechanism traps the system in an echo chamber where, compounded by data sparsity, it fails to capture the dynamic nature of conversational search intents shaped by the open world. As a result, the system skews towards popular but generic suggestions.In this work, we uncover an untapped paradigm shift to shatter this harmful feedback loop: harnessing user "free will" through active user expressions. Unlike traditional recommendations, conversational search empowers users to bypass menus entirely through manually typed queries. The open-world intents in active queries hold the key to breaking this loop. However, incorporating them is non-trivial: (1) there exists an inherent distribution shift between active queries and formulated starters. (2) Furthermore, the "non-ID-able" nature of open text renders traditional item-based popularity statistics ineffective for large-scale industrial streaming training. To this end, we propose Passive-Active Bridge (PA-Bridge), a novel framework that employs an adversarial distribution aligner to bridge the distributional gap between passively recommended starters and active expressions. Moreover, we introduce a semantic discretizer to enable the deployment of popularity debiasing algorithms. Online A/B tests on our platform, demonstrate that PA-Bridge significantly boosts the Feature Penetration Rate by 0.54% and User Active Days

</details>


### [325] [A Case-Driven Multi-Agent Framework for E-Commerce Search Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05991)
*Global E-Commerce Search Relevance Team*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体的自动化框架，通过用自主Agent替代人工角色（标注、优化、用户反馈），实现电商搜索相关性优化的闭环生态系统，包括多轮标注、自主分析解决bad-case、对话式用户交互等模块，并通过集成记忆机制、深度搜索Agent和人类-Agent协作聊天机器人，在实际工业场景中显著提升了标注准确率和bad-case解决效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索相关性优化涉及多个人类角色（用户、产品经理、标注员、算法工程师、评估员），形成一个闭环生态。但由于人工参与存在效率低、耗时长、标注不一致等瓶颈，作者提出能否用自主Agent替代这些人类角色，从而系统性地自动化从bad-case识别到解决的整个流程。

Method: 提出一个案例驱动的多智能体框架，包含三个核心Agent：Annotator Agent（多轮标注）、Optimizer Agent（自主分析并解决bad-case）、User Agent（通过对话交互识别bad-case）。采用harness-engineering范式，构建统一检索排序模型、指令跟随模型用于实时解决、Global Memory减少信息不对称、Deep Search Agent针对低估失效问题，以及基于Agent的聊天机器人实现人机协作。

Result: 广泛的人工评估表明，该框架能有效执行相关性相关任务，提升标注准确率，实现更及时和更具泛化性的bad-case解决。

Conclusion: 该多智能体框架为工业搜索相关性优化提供了一种实用的新范式，通过自动化闭环生态中的多个人类角色，实现了更高效、更准确、更可扩展的相关性优化流程。

Abstract: Relevance is a foundation of user experience in e-commerce search. We view relevance optimization as a closed-loop ecosystem involving multiple human roles: users who provide feedback, product managers who define standards, annotators who label data, algorithm engineers who optimize models, and evaluators who assess performance. Because improving relevance in practice means systematically resolving user-perceived bad cases, we ask a system-level question: can this ecosystem be reimagined by replacing its human roles with autonomous agents? To answer this question, we propose a case-driven multi-agent framework that automates the pipeline from bad-case identification to resolution. The framework instantiates an Annotator Agent for multi-turn annotation, an Optimizer Agent for autonomous bad-case analysis and resolution, and a User Agent that identifies bad cases through conversational interaction, together forming an autonomous and continually evolving system. To make the framework practical in production, we further adopt a harness-engineering paradigm and build a unified retrieval-and-ranking relevance model for efficient training, an instruction-following relevance model for real-time case resolution, Global Memory to reduce information asymmetry across agents, a Deep Search Agent to target underestimation failures, and an agent-based chatbot for human--agent collaboration. Extensive human evaluation shows that the framework performs relevance-related tasks effectively, improves annotation accuracy, and enables more timely and generalizable bad-case resolution, indicating a practical paradigm for industrial search relevance optimization.

</details>


### [326] [OBLIQ-Bench: Exposing Overlooked Bottlenecks in Modern Retrievers with Latent and Implicit Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06235)
*Diane Tchuindjo,Devavrat Shah,Omar Khattab*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文识别了一类被称为"斜向查询"的检索问题，即查询意图隐含潜在模式而非显式关键词。作者构建了OBLIQ-Bench基准测试，包含5个真实长尾语料库上的斜向搜索问题，发现推理型LLM在验证相关文档方面表现优异，但现有检索流程在首次召回相关文档时严重不足，揭示了检索与验证之间的不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索基准日益饱和，但高效搜索远未解决。作者发现一类被忽视的查询——斜向查询，这类查询需要检索隐含潜在模式的文档，而现有方法难以处理。

Method: 识别斜向查询的三种产生机制，构建OBLIQ-Bench基准套件（包含5个斜向搜索问题，覆盖真实长尾语料库），系统对比当前检索流程与推理型LLM在斜向查询上的表现。

Result: 推理型LLM在验证文档相关性方面表现出色，但即使是最先进的检索流程在首次召回相关文档时也表现不佳，存在严重的检索-验证不对称性。

Conclusion: OBLIQ-Bench揭示了现有检索架构在捕捉潜在模式和隐含信号方面的不足，有望推动面向斜向查询的新型检索架构研究。

Abstract: Retrieval benchmarks are increasingly saturating, but we argue that efficient search is far from a solved problem. We identify a class of queries we call oblique, which seek documents that instantiate a latent pattern, like finding all tweets that express an implicit stance, chat logs that demonstrate a particular failure mode, or transcripts that match an abstract scenario. We study three mechanisms through which obliqueness may arise and introduce OBLIQ-Bench, a suite of five oblique search problems over real long-tail corpora. OBLIQ-Bench exposes an overlooked asymmetry between retrieval and verification, where reasoning LLMs reliably recognize latent relevance whenever relevant documents are surfaced, but even sophisticated retrieval pipelines fail to surface most relevant documents in the first place.
